Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [140] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在推荐系统解释中通过行为框架（如社会规范）呈现可持续信息，以助推用户做出更可持续的选择。通过生成式AI生成理论驱动的解释，并在两个领域（低参与度的速溶咖啡和高参与度的酒店预订）进行随机实验（N=529），发现单纯披露可持续信息无效，而行为框架或社会规范显著增加可持续选择并降低决策难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统影响日常消费决策，但如何通过解释机制有效引导可持续消费尚未充分探索。已有研究表明解释影响用户感知，但能否通过行为框架（助推）改变实际选择仍不清楚。

Method: 基于助推理论，使用生成式AI生成可持续感知的解释。通过人工评估和LLM-as-a-judge验证解释质量。在两个领域（速溶咖啡、酒店预订）进行两项随机实验（N=529），参与者从偏好匹配推荐中选择，并接受不同框架的可持续解释。

Result: （1）单纯披露可持续信息不改变选择行为；（2）行为框架或描述性社会规范显著增加可持续选择并降低决策难度；（3）感知与行为分离：单纯披露提升解释评价但未转化为更可持续的选择。

Conclusion: LLM可以规模化生成理论驱动的解释，为基于解释的社会公益干预提供了可行路径。讨论了生成式AI在自适应解释设计中的启示。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [141] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究概念瓶颈模型在医学影像中的应用，发现仅用射血分数（EF）目标训练时，中间概念层（心室容积）的尺度信息会丢失，因为EF作为比值对缩放不变。尽管概念预测精度看似合理，但实际预测的容积值范围严重坍缩。只有在加入绝对单位监督后，容积预测才恢复正常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型通常通过中间概念变量的预测准确度来评判有效性，但本文质疑这种评判是否足够。作者以左心室容积为概念、从超声心动图估计射血分数为例，探究仅靠概念预测准确度能否保证概念层的物理意义。

Method: 使用视频Transformer编码器在公开数据集上训练，收缩末期和舒张末期容积作为概念层，射血分数由容积解析计算（无残差路径）。对比两种训练方式：仅用EF目标 vs. 额外加入容积（毫升）监督。在1276个保留样本上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈未增加EF误差（6.89 vs 7.13 MAE）。但无容积监督时，预测容积范围坍缩至0.1毫升（参考范围35.7和45.7毫升），相关性部分保留。加入绝对单位监督将容积误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，EF误差仅增加0.4。

Conclusion: 仅凭概念准确度可能掩盖缺失物理尺度的概念层。临床模型中的可解释中间变量应根据训练目标的不变性结构进行验证，而非仅依据预测准确度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自推测智能体(Self-Speculating Agent)，将智能体与推测器统一为同一模型，通过联合强化学习方法，在保留任务性能的同时显著提升工具调用预测准确率（Hit@1提升约17个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用推测方法（分离的草稿模型或缓存轨迹）与目标智能体的行为存在对齐偏差（speculator-agent gap），无法有效隐藏工具调用等待延迟。

Method: 提出自推测智能体：同一模型在智能体模式下完成任务，在推测器模式下从部分轨迹预测下一个工具调用，复用前缀KV缓存；采用联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身rollout生成推测目标，交替更新两种模式。

Result: 在智能体搜索QA和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B的Hit@1从44.1提升至61.2，Qwen3.5-4B从48.9提升至66.3，且不损害智能体任务成功率。

Conclusion: 目标智能体本身是强大的下一调用推测器，统一智能体与推测器的设计比分离模型更简单有效，能有效降低工具调用延迟。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [143] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文结合在线学习算法与迭代定价框架，在分布式卫星调度任务中实现99%以上的观测请求满足率，显著优于现有基线方法（87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式约束优化问题（DCOPs）在大规模实际场景中难以整体求解，现有方法在处理大规模卫星调度等真实应用时性能不足。

Method: 1）重新审视DCOPs与势博弈的联系，引入现代在线学习算法进行均衡求解；2）提出层次化分解框架，将大规模DCOP分解为高层元DCOP（任务分配）和底层本地优化（调度），并通过迭代定价方法耦合两层。

Result: 在真实分布式卫星调度问题上，结合在线学习与迭代定价的方法实现了99%+的观测请求满足率，而最先进基线仅为87%。

Conclusion: 在线学习算法与层次化分解框架的组合能有效解决大规模分布式决策问题，特别是在卫星调度等实际场景中取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [144] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill 提出了一种层次化技能图框架，将交互轨迹组织为包含技能节点和原子操作节点的有向图，通过多种类型边（分解、时序、兼容、支持、恢复）连接高层技能与可执行动作，在推理时检索任务相关子图来指导 LLM 智能体执行任务，在三个交互环境中优于基线方法并降低了推理 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法往往将高层文本技能扁平化存储和独立检索，忽略了技能间的关系，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出 HiSkill 框架，构建层次化技能有向图，包含技能节点和 AtomicOp（原子操作）节点，定义分解、时序、兼容、支持、恢复五种类型边。推理时检索任务相关子图，通过符号任务状态、当前技能和子图共同引导 LLM 智能体切换技能、选择原子操作并执行具体动作。

Result: 在三个交互环境中，HiSkill 超越 SOTA 基线方法，同时减少了推理阶段的 token 消耗。

Conclusion: 层次化技能图能够有效弥合高层技能与可执行动作之间的鸿沟，提升 LLM 智能体在长时交互任务中的表现和效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [145] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）用于精算风险建模，通过令牌级对数概率和贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播与量化，在保持基线精算性能的同时提供工作流稳定性与不确定性洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流是高风险的决策支持场景，LLM的概率特性和智能体间依赖会引入不确定性，可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和监管不合规，因此需要有效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，包含数据准备、建模、审查和解释等专业化智能体，由中心枢纽协调。核心创新是利用令牌级对数概率经长度归一化后转化为校准的任务级置信度估计，再通过贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基线精算模型的性能表现，同时额外提供工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的深入洞察。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统可有效支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，对数概率经校准后作为置信度估计再纳入贝叶斯网络是可行的不确定性传播途径。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [146] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHarD，一个基于Pi agent harness构建的可分发安全控制框架，通过在商用AI编程代理上嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三种安全控制，实现了与直接安装相当的安全效果（调整后得分100%），并可通过单条命令分发至分布式用户群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理采用速度极快，但安全风险是组织大规模部署的主要障碍。现有安全控制缺乏系统化分发机制，供应商原生方案存在生态系统依赖问题，不适合所有部署场景。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，在四种代理配置（两种商用代理有/无控制、基础harness、安全加固harness）上，使用源于OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications的23项测试套件进行对比实验。SHarD将OS沙箱、技能扫描、工具限制三类控制嵌入Pi agent harness并通过单命令分发。

Result: SHarD调整后得分100%，与最佳安全配置的商用代理持平，所有测试类别无回归。发现模型非确定性导致安全结果不一致，自主代理行为可跨系统边界，而OS沙箱能直接缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 证明现成的安全控制可通过自定义agent harness嵌入并分发至分布式用户，且效果与直接安装在商用代理上相当。初步提出了控制harness适配性框架的特征，并识别了未来研究的第三研究问题。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [147] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier是一个统一的智能体评估语料库，包含957,253条记录，覆盖30个基准、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器，能够实现跨基准的能力比较与可复用的评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估存在碎片化问题：任务、支架、验证器和评分规则不统一，导致实证结果难以比较。已有工作要么局限于狭窄场景，要么规模有限，或需要昂贵的重新运行。

Method: 收集并标准化了957,253条公共基准记录，补充了六个专业和科学领域的额外运行结果。每个记录按模型、支架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则标准化，并使用SOC/NAICS分类进行职业和行业分析。通过反事实重新评分和构建能力量表进行验证。

Result: (1) 前沿进展在不同基准类型上不均衡：函数调用饱和、编程进步最快、企业工作流最具挑战性；(2) 严格的全通过聚合规则可能掩盖进步并人为改变排名；(3) 构建的能力量表与Epoch的评估能力指数排名Spearman ρ=0.81高度相关。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力量化、基准审计和评估失败分析提供了可复用的基础设施，并可针对领域、职业、动作空间或验证器类型进行专门化。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [148] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"先提议后验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体（IRA），通过调用系统、应用和GUI工具从执行后环境中获取并验证证据，实现了对GUI任务更可靠的自动评估，在GUI-RewardBench基准上达到86.9%的准确率，并成功用于强化学习训练GUI智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的GUI任务评估需要访问环境状态（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等），但现有方法仅依赖执行轨迹截图，难以获取这些关键信息，导致评估不可靠。

Method: 提出IRA（Interactive Reward Agent），基于"提议-验证"框架：先根据任务指令和GUI环境提议任务完成条件，再通过交互式调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具从环境中收集并验证证据。

Result: 在包含321条轨迹的GUI-RewardBench基准上达到86.9%准确率，优于现有基线；用于GUI智能体的强化学习训练后，在OSWorld上取得34.0%的成功率。

Conclusion: IRA通过交互式获取环境状态证据的方式，显著提升了GUI任务评估的可靠性，并能作为有效的奖励信号用于训练GUI智能体。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [149] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentToolMO，一种用于多供应商自治网络中代理工具信任管理的3GPP NRM信息模型，通过标准化跨厂商信任通知、状态机、级联传播和影响评估，实现近实时威胁遏制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在L4-L5自治网络中，AI代理需跨厂商调用工具，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可见性机制，导致一个厂商的工具被攻陷后，其他厂商的代理仍持续调用，引发级联服务影响。

Method: 构建了一个正式定义的信任状态机（含可证明的渐进式强制执行）、阻尼级联传播（有界收敛）、通过现有MnS接口的跨厂商信任通知，以及基于NRM依赖图遍历的追溯影响评估。

Result: 仿真评估表明，标准化跨厂商通知将爆炸半径从小时级未检测传播缩短到近实时遏制（受限于MnS通知交付延迟），级联收敛保证有界迭代次数，通知规模在厂商域间呈次线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架在现有3GPP管理基础设施内运行，利用现有协议，为可信的多厂商自治网络管理提供了标准化路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [150] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope提出了一种针对预训练decoder-only Transformer的高效潜在推理框架，通过将循环计算局限在选定的解码器区间内，并采用渐进式CoT到潜在空间的课程学习，在不增加参数规模或生成长推理链条的情况下，实现了结构化推理任务中的精度与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型进行复杂结构化推理时，要么通过增加参数规模（成本高），要么通过链式思维（CoT）生成长中间步骤（推理计算与生成长度绑定）。作者希望在不增加参数、不生成冗长推理文本的前提下，为模型提供额外的计算能力。

Method: Penelope框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 将解码器分为前缀区间（一次性计算）和循环区间（局部迭代）；(2) 在循环区间内使用时变GRU动态和循环读出状态，迭代精炼问题条件化的边界记忆；(3) 采用CoT到潜在表示的渐进式课程学习，将可见推理逐步迁移到内部循环路径中。

Result: 在开源的结构化推理基准测试上，在验证集选定的潜在预算下，Penelope达到了与现有潜在推理模型相当的准确率，同时降低了测量的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在精炼可以局限在狭窄的解码器区间内，避免重复执行完整解码器或生成长推理链条，为decoder-only Transformer模型提供了一种实用的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [151] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在马尔可夫决策过程的决策树控制器中引入允许的精度损失ε，从而大幅缩小决策树规模（比现有技术小数个数量级），同时保证ε-最优性，实现可调谐的简化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树控制器（如dtControl2）在大型系统或边界情况较多时仍过于复杂，难以被人类理解，而直接缩小树的大小可能导致关键情况遗漏，产生错误控制器。

Method: 在dtControl2基础上扩展"ε"功能：给定允许的不精确度ε≥0，构建更小的决策树，在保证ε-最优性的前提下提炼控制器核心，省略可控量的细节。

Result: 构建的决策树比现有技术小多个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过引入ε机制，在控制器可解释性与最优性之间取得了可控的权衡，使得大规模系统的解释性得到显著提升。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [152] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM是一个多模态图基础模型，通过层次化上下文建模实现零样本迁移，在无目标域微调条件下提升多模态图任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN和LLM方法在处理多模态图的零样本迁移时存在局限：GNN基础模型通常需要下游适配，LLM方法主要处理单模态或单域任务。亟需一种能泛化多模态知识、解耦域特异性的方法。

Method: 提出CHARM模型：用层次化图上下文替代原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系；将域特定节点模式映射到共享高层概念；设计模态感知的图上下文编码器，整合多模态信息与图结构，转换为图token供LLM处理。

Result: 在零样本多模态图任务上取得了一致性改进。

Conclusion: CHARM通过层次化上下文建模有效解耦域特异性和模态特异性，实现了多模态图上的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，一个离线步骤级基准测试，用于评估桌面计算机使用代理（CUA）在动作后状态验证、源追踪和上下文感知控制方面的能力。包含 2,013 个人工验证实例，涵盖时间排序和前后对比两种任务。实验发现，即便最优模型在排序任务上的精确匹配率也仅约 65%，且上下文信息对不同任务的影响存在矛盾。DDB 填补了 GUI 基础定位与最终任务成功之间的诊断空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注最终任务成功率或单帧基础定位，无法评估模型是否能重建动作产生的因果、任务相关的状态转换——这对于拒绝过时观察、验证进展和从失败中恢复至关重要。

Method: 构建 DDB 基准，包含 2,013 个人工验证实例，来自约 15 个应用和 50 个任务域的跨应用 Linux 轨迹。设计两种互补任务：463 个 3 帧时间排序实例（含 105 个跨轨迹干扰项）和 1,550 个前后对比对（标注 5 种动作及其载荷）。评估 8 个模型家族在 32 种排序和 16 种单动作设置下的表现。

Result: 排序任务最佳非干扰项和干扰项精确匹配率分别为 65.1% 和 65.7%。任务上下文使干扰项识别提升 6.9 个百分点，但使非干扰项精确匹配下降 2.2 个百分点；错误分析显示模型系统性复制 A-B-C 顺序。单动作任务中，推断动作类型比定位更难：点击 F1 为 0.96，拖动仅为 0.76，但识别出的拖动通常定位良好。

Conclusion: DDB 作为端到端基准的补充，填补了 GUI 基础定位与最终任务成功之间缺失的诊断层，能够针对性地改进桌面 CUA 的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Ultra-Low-Energy Open-Circuit Fault Diagnosis for Three-Phase Inverters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25037)
*Xiaoyi Lei,Fanfu Wu,Yunting Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的嵌入式三相逆变器故障诊断方法，相比传统CNN在GPU上实现，推理能耗降低382倍（每次诊断仅11微焦耳），同时保持100%的诊断准确率，并验证了在非平衡负载、电流幅值变化和测量噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN-based故障诊断方法在嵌入式环境中计算密集、推理能耗高，无法满足三相逆变器控制硬件亚瓦特级的功耗预算。

Method: 将基于电流矢量轨迹矩阵训练的CNN转换为脉冲神经网络（SNN），利用轨迹矩阵的稀疏结构仅在信息区域激活计算，通过NengoLoihi框架和Loihi神经形态芯片进行能耗评估。

Result: 每次诊断仅需11微焦耳，相比GPU上的CNN推理能耗降低382倍，准确率保持100%，在非平衡负载、电流幅值阶跃变化和测量噪声下均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的神经形态框架在嵌入式逆变器故障诊断中实现了极低能耗与高精度的兼顾，为资源受限的电力电子系统提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Embedded fault diagnosis in three-phase inverters must satisfy the sub-watt power budget of converter control hardware, but conventional convolutional neural network (CNN)-based methods require dense multiply-accumulate operations and impose substantial inference energy. This work proposes an event-driven neuromorphic framework for energy-efficient open-circuit (OC) fault diagnosis. A CNN trained on current-vector trajectory matrices is converted into a spiking neural network (SNN) and evaluated using the NengoLoihi framework with Loihi-based neuromorphic energy estimation. By exploiting the sparse structure of trajectory matrices, the SNN activates computation only in informative regions instead of processing the full feature map densely. Experiments on a three-phase inverter platform show that the proposed method achieves 11 microjoules per diagnosis, corresponding to a 382 times inference-energy reduction compared with a GPU-based CNN, while maintaining 100% diagnostic accuracy. Robustness is further validated under unbalanced loading, current amplitude step changes, and injected measurement noise.

</details>


### [155] [Integrated Eco-Driving and Powertrain Optimization for Hybrid Vehicles in Complex Urban Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25114)
*Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian,Mehdi Farasat*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向城市混合动力汽车的集成式有限时域生态驾驶规划框架，通过统一的混合整数规划形式同时优化纵向运动、车道选择、交叉路口行为与动力总成操作，仿真结果表明该方法在保证安全、舒适与交通规则合规的前提下提升了能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市生态驾驶研究通常只优化纵向运动、换道、交叉路口通行或动力总成能量管理等单一或部分决策，缺乏对上述所有方面进行联合优化的统一框架。

Method: 构建集成式有限时域规划框架，采用统一的混合整数规划（mixed-integer formulation）对纵向运动、车道选择与变更、跟车与换道安全、信号灯与非信号灯路口行为以及混合动力总成操作进行联合建模。

Result: 与规则跟随、信号感知、允许超车及纯运动学优化等基线方法相比，所提方法在保证可行、安全、舒适且符合交通规则的前提下，取得了更优的能量性能。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将交通行为与动力总成操作进行联合优化的可行性与有效性，为城市混合动力汽车实现更全面的生态驾驶提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Urban eco-driving requires the simultaneous planning of speed, acceleration, lane decisions, surrounding-vehicle safety, intersection rules, and vehicle energy use. Existing studies commonly optimize only selected aspects of urban driving, such as longitudinal motion, lane changing, intersection crossing, or powertrain energy management, while treating the remaining decisions separately. Unlike these studies, this paper develops an integrated finite-horizon eco-driving planning framework for urban hybrid vehicles that jointly optimizes traffic behavior and powertrain operation within a unified mixed-integer formulation. The novelty of the proposed method lies in simultaneously modeling longitudinal motion, lane occupancy and changes, car-following and lane-change safety, signalized and unsignalized intersection behavior, and hybrid powertrain operation. The formulation captures lane availability, lane-dependent speed limits, mandatory and emergency lane changes, safe-to-clear signal conditions, downstream clearance, stop-and-yield rules, engine and motor power, battery state of charge, regenerative braking, and fuel consumption. Simulation studies compare the proposed method with rule-following, signal-aware, overtaking-enabled, and kinematic-only optimization baselines. The results demonstrate improved energy performance while maintaining feasible, safe, comfortable, and traffic-rule-compliant urban driving plans.

</details>


### [156] [VeraGrid-Agent: Tool-Augmented LLMs for Distribution Optimal Power Flow at the Grid Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25155)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Hamed Mohsenian-Rad,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出VeraGrid-Agent，一种工具增强型LLM，能自主编写仿真器输入、执行开源VeraGrid求解器并读取输出，在D-OPF问题上准确率从无工具时的42.7%-49.3%提升至97.3%-100.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在回答复杂科学问题（如配电网最优潮流）时，仅靠参数化知识的语言推理常给出错误答案，因此需要引入数值求解器和仿真器来提升准确性。

Method: 提出VeraGrid-Agent，一种工具增强型LLM，能自主编写仿真器输入、执行开源VeraGrid求解器、读取输出后作答；并构建VeraGrid-MCQ-150数据集进行评估。

Result: 无工具时模型准确率仅42.7%-49.3%；使用VeraGrid-Agent后准确率提升至97.3%-100.0%。

Conclusion: 工具增强（仿真器接入）能显著提升LLM在复杂科学问题上的回答准确性，剩余错误主要源于多步推理中的语义理解偏差，而非仿真执行失败。

Abstract: Language models have demonstrated remarkable success in solving a wide range of tasks. However, answering complex scientific questions about the power flow often requires solving the distribution optimal power flow (D-OPF) problem. These questions call for numerical solvers and simulators, as linguistic reasoning from parametric knowledge often gives incorrect answers. In this work, we present VeraGrid-Agent, a tool-augmented LLM that autonomously writes the simulator input, executes the open-source VeraGrid solver, and reads the solver output before answering. To evaluate performance, we introduce VeraGrid-MCQ-150, a set of deterministic, expert template driven, $150$ multiple-choice questions. We evaluate the performance under two regimes: (i) no-tool reasoning and (ii) agent (LLM with simulator access). Without tools, every model performs with an accuracy of $42.7\%$--$49.3\%$. However, with VeraGrid-Agent, accuracy increases to $97.3\%$--$100.0\%$. We also do a failure-mode analysis to show that the few remaining errors arise from wrong interpretations during multi-step reasoning, rather than any failure in the simulators execution.

</details>


### [157] [Transient Synchronization Stability Analysis of SG-DFIG Parallel System Considering Complete LVRT Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25204)
*Hongsheng Xu,Meng Zhan,Jia Hu,Huiyu Shang,Qingfeng Yue*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对含同步发电机（SG）和双馈感应发电机（DFIG）的混合电力系统，基于DFIG的完整低电压穿越（LVRT）过程，将暂态过程划分为四个阶段，推导出统一广义摆动方程（GSE），并提出改进等面积定则来评估暂态同步稳定性（TSS），通过硬件在环实验验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大量工作致力于电磁暂态仿真分析含可再生能源和同步发电机的混合系统的暂态同步稳定性，但考虑可再生能源设备完整低电压穿越过程的底层机制尚待研究。

Method: 以SG-DFIG并联系统为对象，基于DFIG的LVRT将暂态过程划分为故障前、故障中、故障后早期和故障后晚期四个阶段；建立描述完整四阶段LVRT过程的暂态模型；推导各阶段统一的广义摆动方程（GSE）；提出考虑频率跳变和非线性阻尼的改进等面积定则。

Result: 发现绝大多数故障会导致DFIG进入LVRT并使并联系统呈现顺序切换特性；推导了不同阶段统一的GSE；改进的等面积定则有效评估TSS；硬件在环实验和仿真验证了理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为考虑完整LVRT过程的混合系统TSS机制提供了更清晰的物理图像，并与传统SG主导电力系统的暂态稳定性建立了更紧密的联系。

Abstract: Although a large amount of work has been devoted to detailed electromagnetic transient simulation in analyzing transient synchronization stability (TSS) of hybrid systems containing renewable energy equipment and synchronous generator (SG), the underlying mechanism considering complete low-voltage ride-through (LVRT) processes of renewable energy equipment remains to be studied. Taking the SG and doubly fed induction generator (SG-DFIG) parallel system as an objective, this work divides its transient processes into four different stages: pre-fault (stage 1), during-fault (stage 2), early post-fault (stage 3), and late post-fault (stage 4), based on the LVRT of the DFIG, and establishes a transient model to describe the complete 4-stage LVRT processes. By studying the condition for entering the LVRT, it is found that vast majority of faults can cause the DFIG to enter the LVRT and make the parallel system exhibit the sequential switching characteristics. Similar to the SG-SG parallel system, which can be reduced to a single SG and described by a second-order swing equation, a unified generalized swing equation (GSE) under different parameters for different stages 1, 2, and 3 is derived. Therefore, the transient stability of the parallel system can be dealt with easily, and further, an improved equal area criterion method considering two additional effects of frequency jump and nonlinear damping is proposed to evaluate the TSS. These GSE-based theoretical analysis results are all supported by extensive hardware-in-the-loop experiments and simulations. Obviously, this work provides a clearer physical picture for the TSS mechanism of the hybrid system considering complete LVRT processes, and makes a closer connection with the transient stability of traditional power systems dominated by SG.

</details>


### [158] [Progressive Replacement Sequence Planning for Laser-Enhanced BeiDou Navigation Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25235)
*Huan Yan,Juan A. Fraire,Ziqi Yang,Kanglian Zhao,Wenfeng Li,Jinjun Zheng,Ziyu Wang,Changgang Zheng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了北斗三号（BDS-3）卫星渐进替换规划问题，提出了一种网络感知的激光-微波混合星座替换序列优化方法，通过整数线性规划（ILP）模型和优先感知搜索启发式算法，实现了比历史发射顺序更优的混合网络演化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDS-3早期卫星即将达到设计寿命，渐进式星座替换不可避免；同时激光星间链路（LISL）具备高精度时间传递、亚毫米级测距等优势，为未来北斗卫星升级提供了新路径。因此，需要从组网角度规划如何用激光卫星逐步替换传统微波卫星。

Method: (1) 将北斗渐进替换建模为网络感知的发射序列规划问题，考虑激光-微波混合星座演化状态；(2) 建立整数线性规划（ILP）模型，评估候选发射行动在工程约束下的组网增益；(3) 引入优先感知搜索启发式算法，缩减行动评估空间以实现高效的逐轮决策。

Result: 基于实际BDS-3星座的仿真表明，所提替换序列相较于历史发射顺序，能更早形成地面可接入的激光增强网络结构，更充分利用激光链路资源，减少残余非锚定卫星数量，降低微波层等待时延。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为未来北斗演进的网络感知决策支持工具，生成的替换序列可直接为北斗替换规划提供工程指导。

Abstract: The early satellites of the third-generation BeiDou Navigation Satellite System (BDS-3) are approaching the end of their design lifetime, making progressive constellation replacement an inevitable engineering task. Meanwhile, laser inter-satellite links (LISLs) provide high-precision time transfer, sub-millimeter-level ranging, and high-rate data forwarding capabilities, offering a promising upgrade path for future BeiDou satellites. This paper investigates the replacement-sequence planning problem for progressively replacing legacy microwave satellites with laser-enabled satellites from a networking perspective. To address this problem, three main contributions are made: (i) BeiDou progressive replacement is formulated as a network-aware launch sequence planning problem over evolving laser--microwave hybrid constellation states; (ii) an integer linear programming (ILP) model is developed to evaluate the networking gain of each candidate launch action under practical engineering constraints; and (iii) a priority-aware search heuristic is introduced to reduce the action evaluation space for efficient round-wise decision making. Simulations based on the actual BDS-3 constellation demonstrate that the proposed replacement sequence achieves a more favorable hybrid-network evolution than the historical BDS-3 launch order, including earlier formation of a ground-accessible laser-enhanced network structure, better utilization of laser-link resources, fewer residual non-anchor satellites, and lower microwave-layer waiting delay. The proposed framework can serve as a network-aware decision-support tool for future BeiDou evolution, and the resulting replacement sequence can provide direct engineering guidance for BeiDou replacement planning.

</details>


### [159] [MR-TGN: A Meta-Role Temporal Graph Network for Team-Level Intent Prediction in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25316)
*Nagarani Brammanayagam,Devaprakash Muniraj*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出元角色时序图网络（MR-TGN）框架，通过动态图建模、时序记忆机制和隐式元角色学习，在部分可观测的动态多智能体系统中实现早期集体意图预测，实验表明该方法优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中集体意图预测面临复杂交互、演化合作结构、长期行为依赖等挑战，且智能体的功能角色通常是潜在的、随时间变化的，缺乏明确标注，导致学习协调群体行为极为困难。

Method: 提出MR-TGN框架：1）将智能体建模为动态演化图实体；2）使用时序记忆机制编码历史交互与协调行为；3）引入记忆增强的元角色学习机制，无需显式角色标签即可从智能体行为表征中推导潜在角色表示；4）提出早期集体意图预测评估方法，兼顾准确性与及时性。

Result: 在代表性多智能体场景中，MR-TGN在动态对抗环境下持续优于竞争基线，实现了有效的早期集体意图预测。

Conclusion: MR-TGN通过隐式学习动态角色表征和时序图建模，有效解决了多智能体系统中集体意图预测的核心难题，尤其在早期预测阶段展现出优势。

Abstract: Collective intent prediction in multi-agent systems focuses on predicting the shared objectives and future behaviours of groups of interacting agents. The problem is particularly challenging because collective intent emerges from complex interactions, evolving cooperation structures, and long-term behavioural dependencies among heterogeneous agents operating in dynamic and partially observable environments. Furthermore, functional roles adopted by agents are often latent, may change over time, and are rarely available as explicit annotations, making the learning of coordinated group behaviours significantly more difficult. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a Meta-Role Temporal Graph Network (MR-TGN) framework for collective intent prediction in multi-agent systems. The proposed framework models agents as dynamically evolving graph entities and employs temporal memory mechanisms to encode historical interactions and coordination behaviours. To capture higher-level behavioural knowledge, MR-TGN introduces a memory-enhanced meta-role learning mechanism that derives latent role representations from agent-centric behavioral representations without requiring explicit role labels. An evaluation methodology for early collective intent prediction is proposed to assess prediction accuracy and timeliness, enabling realistic evaluation of the model's ability to anticipate collective objectives during the early stages of mission execution. Experimental results on representative multi-agent scenarios demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves effective early prediction of collective intents in dynamic and adversarial environments.

</details>


### [160] [Prosumer-Centric Flexible Dynamic Operating Envelopes for Low-Voltage Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25328)
*Abhishek Mishra,Ashish R. Hota,Gayan Lankeshwara,Rahul Sharma,Wayes Tushar,Prabodh Bajpai*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种灵活动态运行包络（Flexible DOE）方法，通过上下限约束代替单点限值，在满足电网约束的同时减少分布式能源的削减和运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态运行包络（DOE）方法要求产消者上报期望功率，然后计算不超出上报值的限制，这会导致可再生能源和负荷因不确定性被不必要地削减。本文旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 采用灵活优化框架，为每个产消者计算有功和无功功率交换的上下限（而非单一限值），在保证网络约束的前提下尽可能包络上报值，在最优性与灵活性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在改进的澳大利亚低压配电网上的验证表明，相比非灵活DOE，本文方法能显著减少功率削减和总运营成本，同时始终将电压幅值维持在期望范围内。

Conclusion: 灵活DOE通过上下限约束的设计，能够有效应对分布式能源的不确定性，在保障电网安全的同时提升经济性和可再生能源利用率。

Abstract: As the share of distributed energy resources (DERs) increases in the distribution network, maintaining compliance with network constraints has become a challenge. In this context, dynamic operating envelopes (DOEs) have emerged as a promising solution, where the distribution network operator (DNO) computes and imposes a dynamically varying import and export limit on power exchange between prosumers and the distribution network. Existing DOE approaches often require the prosumers to report their desired power exchange to the DNO, which then computes DOE limits that typically do not exceed the reported values. However, due to uncertain renewable generation and load demand, such DOE limits can potentially result in unnecessary curtailment of generation and load during real-time operation. This work addresses this limitation by computing flexible DOEs using a flexible optimization framework that trades off optimality with flexibility. The proposed approach computes both upper and lower limits on the active and reactive power exchange between each prosumer and the grid, and tries to contain the reported values between the upper and lower limits. As long as the power exchange resides within the DOE limits, network constraints are satisfied. The proposed flexible DOE is validated on a modified Australian low-voltage distribution network. Compared to non-flexible DOE, the proposed framework demonstrates superior performance in reducing curtailment and total operational costs while consistently maintaining voltage magnitudes within desired limits.

</details>


### [161] [An Interaction Sensitivity Framework in Perturbation Space for the Converter-Driven Stability of Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25334)
*Jihun Kook,Jung-Wook Park*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扰动空间的交互灵敏度框架，用于分析高维动力网络中模式与状态之间的扰动驱动交互重构，超越了传统参与因子分析无法处理扰动后交互变化的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参与因子（PF）分析仅适用于固定运行点，无法捕捉扰动发生后模式与状态之间交互关系的重构，而这对于理解现代电力系统中由变流器接口发电主导的多振荡现象及其级联脱网风险至关重要。

Method: 建立了一个在扰动空间中定义的交互灵敏度框架，提供解析表达式和物理解释机制，揭示模式和状态如何响应扰动，并阐明控制调整为何增强或削弱稳定性的因果关系。

Result: 该框架能够解释扰动后模式-状态交互的重构机制，提供超越传统稳定性分析的因果解释，并适用于任意由状态空间方程描述的高维动力网络。

Conclusion: 该交互灵敏度框架为理解扰动驱动的交互重构提供了通用的解析基础，支持复杂系统中先进控制器的设计。

Abstract: As the integration of renewables accelerates the decarbonization process in the energy sector, power systems are becoming increasingly dominated by converter-interfaced generation. However, this results in the emergence of multiple oscillations, which can trigger cascading disconnections and potentially damage generators. To address these critical issues, modern power systems commonly employ classic participation factor (PF) analyses, which relate oscillatory modes (eigenvalues) to controller state variables. While these methods are effective, a critical yet previously unexplored challenge involves capturing the interaction reconfigurations observed after a perturbation occurs. Because PF analyses are limited to fixed operating points, they are structurally unable to analyze these event-dependent mode--state interactions under perturbations. Here, we establish an interaction sensitivity framework that is formulated in perturbation space, uncovering the mechanisms by which modes and states respond to perturbations. This framework provides analytic expressions and physically interpretable mechanisms that explain why specific control adjustments enhance or reduce stability, revealing causal relationships beyond conventional stability analysis. More broadly, this formulation provides a general analytical basis for understanding perturbation-driven interaction reconfigurations in high-dimensional dynamical networks described by state-space equations, and supports advanced controller design in complex systems.

</details>


### [162] [Critical slowing down for predicting controller induced loss of control in quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25370)
*Jasper J. van Beers,Prashant Solanki,Erik-Jan van Kampen,Coen C. de Visser*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于临界减速(Critical Slowing Down, CSD)的新型四旋翼飞行器失控(LOC)预测方案。该方案无需系统模型，在真实飞行数据上实现了高达0.9秒的提前预警，在检测准确率和数据依赖方面优于现有基于RNN的方法，并能跨控制器架构和飞行场景泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼飞行器因控制器不稳定导致的失控(LOC)是一个严重的安全问题，现有预测方法通常依赖系统模型或需要大量LOC数据。作者希望开发一种无需系统模型、不依赖LOC事件数据、且能跨场景泛化的通用预测方法。

Method: 利用临界减速(CSD)这一跨生态和生物系统的临界转变通用前兆现象，提取早期预警信号，无需系统模型即可进行LOC预测。在四架不同四旋翼的真实LOC飞行数据上进行评估，并与状态空间RNN方法对比。

Result: 实现了高达0.9秒的提前预警时间；在检测准确率和数据依赖方面优于现有RNN方法；无需LOC事件数据即可准确预测；无需重新参数化即可泛化到不同控制器架构、不同四旋翼平台和不同LOC场景（室内外飞行）。

Conclusion: 基于CSD的预测方案是一种通用、有效且可泛化的四旋翼LOC预测方法，打破了传统方法对系统模型和LOC数据的依赖，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a novel forecasting scheme to anticipate controller induced loss of control (LOC) in quadrotors and evaluate it on real LOC flight data from four different quadrotors. For this, early warning signals of LOC are derived using critical slowing down (CSD), a generic phenomenon shown to precede critical transitions across various complex ecological and biological systems. As such, our early warning indicators are generic in the sense that no system models are needed to facilitate forecasts of LOC. The approach is evaluated on real quadrotor flight data wherein LOC occurs due to unstable controller behavior arising from input-output delays. Our approach achieves a time-to-LOC forecast of up to 0.9 seconds before LOC occurs, outperforming state-of-the-art recurrent neural network quadrotor LOC forecasters in terms of detection accuracy and LOC data reliance. In particular, we leverage insights from CSD to accurately predict LOC without using data of the LOC event itself. Going further, we apply our forecasters without any re-parameterization to anticipate a different LOC scenario, quadrotor flyways, that occur on other quadrotors flying both indoors and outdoors. Despite these differences, our approach successfully detects LOC, demonstrating that it can generalize across controller architectures, quadrotors, and LOC scenarios.

</details>


### [163] [A Hierarchical Stochastic Model Predictive Control Framework for Integrated Request-aware Charge Scheduling and Service Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25421)
*Mainak Dan,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对灵活A-to-B租赁模式下MoD-EV的集成充电与服务分配统一控制机制，采用多目标机会约束MILP-MPC框架，结合分段线性电池模型和混合逻辑切换机制。相比BAU和LC方法，该方法显著降低了充电成本和电池容量退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoD-EV的充电操作受随机客户租赁请求和时变电价影响，现有方法未能有效统一优化充电与服务分配，且非线性电池动态带来计算挑战。

Method: 采用分段线性模型逼近非线性电池动态，构建多目标机会约束MILP-MPC框架，利用混合逻辑切换机制确定最优充电序列，并采用分布式方法提升计算可扩展性。

Result: 与BAU和LC方法相比，该方法在不同置信水平下均显著降低了充电成本和电池容量退化。

Conclusion: 集成充电与服务分配的统一控制机制相比现有方法具有显著优势，可提升MoD-EV系统的成本效益和电池健康。

Abstract: This study introduces a unified control mechanism for integrated charging and service allocation of Mobility-on-Demand Electric Vehicles (MoD-EVs) operating under a flexible A-to-B rental model. The charging operation of MoD-EVs is influenced by stochastic customer rental requests and time-varying electricity prices. The framework captures the nonlinear battery dynamics and optimizes the charging operations against dynamic electricity prices and to meet stochastic customer demand while prioritizing battery health and cost efficiency. To address the computational challenges posed by the nonlinear battery dynamics, the framework employs a piece-wise linear model integrated into a multi-objective, chance-constrained mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) Model Predictive Control (MPC) formulation. A mixed logical switching mechanism is utilized to determine optimal charging sequences. Furthermore, a distributed approach is implemented to ensure computational scalability compared to centralized alternatives. Evaluation of this approach using a state-of-the-art commercial solver with stochastic EV rental requests under different confidence levels and time-varying electricity prices demonstrates significant benefits of the integrated mechanism design, including a reduction in charging costs and battery capacity degradation compared to the prevailing business-as-usual (BAU) approach and state of the art Laxity-based charging (LC) approach.

</details>


### [164] [Integrated Framework for Long-term Elective Surgery Management under Uncertainty: From Strategic to Tactical Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25482)
*Joao P. F. da Silva,Lucas Bortoletto,Rafael C. S. Schouery,Edilson F. Arruda,Fabricio F. Santos,Ana Paula C. I. Alves*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成框架，将战略层面的队列控制决策与战术层面的手术室排程相连接，以解决公立医疗系统中择期手术等待名单的管理难题。框架包含闭环控制策略、加班取消风险模型和混合整数线性规划三个组件，在巴西大型公立医院的案例验证中展现出稳定等待名单、控制取消风险且计算高效的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公立医疗系统中择期手术等待名单长期存在管理挑战，医院需在有限的手术室容量与不确定且不断变化的需求之间取得平衡。现有模型往往忽略患者到达时间和手术时长的随机性，且孤立的资源分配决策难以长期稳定等待名单。

Method: 提出三组件集成框架：(i) 闭环控制策略，确定每个规划周期应安排的患者数量；(ii) 加班相关取消风险模型；(iii) 受前两个组件约束的混合整数线性规划模型，用于求解手术室排程问题。通过巴西坎皮纳斯州立大学医院实际案例进行评估。

Result: 集成战略与战术决策能够稳定等待名单、控制取消风险，支持更透明的手术室资源分配决策，且计算效率足以支撑实际应用。

Conclusion: 将战略队列控制与战术手术室排程相融合是管理择期手术等待名单的有效途径，可兼顾长期稳定性和短期可操作性，值得在类似公立医疗系统中推广应用。

Abstract: In public healthcare systems, elective surgery waiting lists are a persistent management challenge, as hospitals must balance limited operating theatre capacity with uncertain and evolving demand. Long-term planning for these lists requires more than assigning operating theatre (OT) time to medical specialities, as isolated allocation decisions may fail to stabilise waiting lists over time. Such planning must account for uncertainty in patient arrivals in the queue and surgery durations, which are often neglected in OT scheduling models. In this context, we propose a novel integrated framework for elective surgery management under uncertainty, linking strategic queue-control decisions with tactical OT scheduling. The framework combines three components: (i) a closed-loop control policy that determines the number of patients to schedule in each planning cycle; (ii) a model to account for overtime-related cancellation risks; and (iii) a mixed-integer linear programming formulation, informed by the previous components, to solve an OT Scheduling Problem. The resulting tactical schedules are therefore guided by the long-term evolution of speciality-specific queues and by cancellation-risk considerations. We evaluate the proposed framework through a case study at University Hospital Unicamp, a large Brazilian public referral hospital. The results show that integrating strategic and tactical decisions stabilises waiting lists, controls cancellation risks, and supports more transparent theatre-allocation decisions, while remaining computationally efficient for practical implementation.

</details>


### [165] [To What Extent Can Inherent Communication Noise Guarantee Privacy in Distributed Cooperative Control?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25564)
*Yuwen Ma,Sarah Spurgeon,Tao Li,Boli Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多智能体系统中固有通信噪声实现差分隐私的分布式协同控制方案，无需额外注入人工噪声，并设计了有限时域LQR机制实现编队跟踪与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需主动注入人工噪声来保护隐私，本文探索能否利用通信中固有的物理噪声（发射机扰动、接收机噪声、路径损耗等）作为天然隐私机制，以降低对系统性能的额外影响。

Method: 建立基于物理的通信噪声模型（包含距离依赖的噪声方差），设计分布式有限时域LQR控制器，提出以LQR权重矩阵比值作为隐私保护对象，并采用集合论敏感性分析来推导隐私界。

Result: 在无需人工噪声注入的条件下，所提机制能在无限时域内提供有界的累积(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证；编队跟踪误差几乎必然收敛且均方收敛到有限随机极限，期望有界。

Conclusion: 通信固有噪声可作为一种有效的隐私保护机制，在隐私与性能之间取得可量化的权衡，且权重比值形式的隐私定义比梯度保护方法更宽松。

Abstract: This paper proposes a differentially private distributed cooperative control scheme for multi-agent systems (MAS). Unlike conventional approaches that actively inject artificial noise for privacy protection, this work investigates whether inherent communication noise can itself serve as a natural privacy mechanism. A physically motivated communication-noise model is developed for mobile MAS by incorporating transmitter perturbation, receiver noise, path-loss attenuation, and log-normal shadowing. The resulting effective noise variance depends on inter-agent state differences, thereby capturing the distance-dependent signal perturbation arising in practice. Based on this model, a distributed finite-horizon Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) mechanism is designed to achieve formation tracking while protecting agents' private control preferences. Rather than protecting the full local cost function, the proposed privacy formulation focuses on the ratio of the LQR weighting matrices, which captures the trade-off between tracking accuracy and control effort when the quadratic cost structure is publicly known. A set-theoretic sensitivity analysis shows that this weighting-ratio adjacency formulation yields less conservative privacy bounds than gradient-based protection under the considered addition/removal adjacency relation. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that, under suitable design conditions, the proposed mechanism provides bounded cumulative (ε,δ)-differential privacy guarantees for the weighting ratios over an infinite horizon without artificial noise injection. Meanwhile, the cooperative tracking error is shown to converge almost surely and in mean square to a finite random limit, with its expectation remaining bounded. Numerical examples validate the theoretical results and illustrate the resulting privacy-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [166] [A Minimal Dynamical Model for Incubation-Outbreak Transitions in Social Norm Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25586)
*Run Wang,Leonardo Cianfanelli,Giacomo Como,Wenjun Mei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三状态社会规范扩散的最小动力学模型（非支持者、沉默支持者、 vocal倡导者），发现了一种潜伏-爆发模式：初始小规模采纳后经历长期平静期，随后突然内生性爆发。通过完全解析分析，揭示了由内部互动而非外部冲击驱动社会突变的动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社会规范扩散中为何会出现"潜伏期"现象——即看似平静的表面下积累着支持，最终突然爆发——并且证明这种突变可以由系统内部动力学自发产生，不需要外部冲击或"引爆点"式触发。

Method: 构建了一个三状态（非支持者→沉默支持者→ vocal倡导者）的最小动力学模型，与经典SI/SIS模型有密切关联但表现出更丰富的动态。通过平衡点分析、稳定性分析、收敛性分析和相变条件分析，完整刻画了系统动力学行为，并推导了潜伏期的解析估计。

Result: 发现了潜伏-爆发的非平凡动态模式：初始小波采纳→长期平静期→突然内生性爆发。完成了平衡态、稳定性、收敛性和相变条件的完整解析刻画，识别出产生孵化现象的非线性机制，并给出了潜伏期的理论估计。

Conclusion: 社会突变可以通过纯粹的内部相互作用动力学产生，无需依赖外部冲击。该模型为理解"沉默的多数"如何转变为"发声的多数"提供了理论框架，对解释政治变革、文化规范转变等社会现象具有启示意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a minimal dynamical model for the diffusion of a new social norm, in which individuals transition among three states: non-supporters, silent supporters, and vocal advocates. Despite its simplicity and its close relation to the classical SI-type and SIS-type spreading dynamics, this model exhibits a nontrivial latent-outbreak dynamic pattern: an initial small adoption wave is followed by a long quiescent period and then an abrupt, endogenous explosion of support. Through a complete analytical characterization of equilibria, stability, convergence, and phase-transition conditions, we identify the nonlinear mechanism that generates this incubation phenomenon and derive estimates of the latent period. Our results reveal a dynamical route to sudden social change driven purely by internal interactions rather than external shocks.

</details>


### [167] [How haptic feedback enables human group synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25692)
*Angelo Di Porzio,Etienne Burdet,Marco Coraggio*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究测试了触觉通信在群体运动任务中诱导或增强同步的能力，发现即使弱触觉耦合也能引发群体同步，且触觉与视觉结合效果优于单一模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证触觉通信能否在群体运动中诱导或增强同步，弥补视觉交互受传感器延迟、遮挡和注意力限制的不足。

Method: 四组四人团队通过虚拟弹性带连接，在四种条件（仅触觉、仅视觉、触觉+视觉、无反馈）下执行手腕屈伸振荡运动。

Result: 弱触觉耦合即能诱导群体同步；触觉+视觉同步性更高且更稳定；触觉反馈提高协调频率，运动平滑度随频率增加。

Conclusion: 为设计群体协作环境中的触觉交互协议奠定基础。

Abstract: Synchronization often emerges spontaneously among interacting people, yielding practical benefits for tasks such as sports, physical rehabilitation, and collaborative manufacturing, and fostering a sense of unity and trust. Although visual interaction is typically considered the primary channel for achieving synchronization, it is constrained by sensorimotor delays, occlusions, and limited attentional resources. Here, we tested whether haptic communication can induce or enhance synchronization in a group motor task. Six quartets performed oscillatory wrist flexion/extension while being connected via virtual elastic bands; we compared haptic only, visual only, combined haptic\&visual, and no feedback conditions. Even weak haptic coupling induced group synchronization, and combining haptic with visual feedback yielded higher and more stable synchronization than either modality alone. Haptic feedback also increased the frequency at which groups coordinated, and movement smoothness rose with frequency. These findings lay the groundwork for designing haptic interaction protocols for collaborative group environments.

</details>


### [168] [Joint identification of permanent magnet synchronous machine and inverter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25739)
*Giulio Montecchio,Sven Reimann,Benjamin Hartmann,Maximilian Manderla,Jan Achterhold,Daniel Görges*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需预先标定逆变器参数，通过联合辨识磁链图与逆变器简化模型，利用张量积样条模型在dq坐标系下提高离线磁链辨识精度的方法，并在FEM仿真与测试台数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电驱建模中，磁链图辨识对扭矩预测、控制器参数化和仿真建模至关重要，但控制器输出电压受逆变器非线性扰动影响，传统方法需先辨识逆变器参数，过程繁琐。

Method: 在dq参考系下将磁链图表示为dq磁链与dq电流间的静态关系，采用张量积样条模型捕捉饱和与交叉饱和效应；将磁链图与逆变器简化模型进行联合辨识，无需预识逆变器参数。

Result: 在FEM仿真和测试台数据上验证，表明电压扰动对磁链估计不可忽略，联合辨识可显著改善磁链模型精度。

Conclusion: 联合辨识磁链图与逆变器模型能有效提升离线磁链辨识精度，避免了先验逆变器参数辨识的需求。

Abstract: In electric drive modeling, identifying the magnetic flux maps is essential for predicting accurately the torque, parameterizing a controller for tracking the torque or creating a simulation model. However, the voltage output by the controller (commanded voltage) is usually disturbed by non-linearity of the inverter, which needs to be taken into account. This paper presents a novel approach to enhance the offline flux identification from commanded voltage inputs, circumventing the need for prior identification of inverter parameters. In the dq reference frame, the flux maps are represented as static relationships between dq fluxes and dq currents. We utilize a tensor product spline model to accurately capture saturation and cross-saturation effects. The effects of the voltage disturbance on the estimated flux maps are not negligible. It is demonstrated that a joint identification of the flux maps and a simple model of the inverter can highly improve the flux model. The method is validated on FEM simulation and test-bench data.

</details>


### [169] [A Hierarchical Optimisation Framework for Integrated Electric-Hydrogen-Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25776)
*Fulong Yao,Yiming Xu,Liana Cipcigan,Maurizio Albano,Naeima Hamed,Nima Valizadeh,Omer Rana*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层优化框架，用于集成电-氢-交通系统（EHTS）。上层采用无求解器的贪婪启发式算法（SFGH）实现车辆实时调度，下层基于深度强化学习（DRL）优化能源调度，实验验证了框架的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车和氢燃料汽车的普及使得电-氢-交通系统高度耦合，车辆调度与多能源调度之间的强耦合带来了显著的运行挑战，亟需一种高效实时的协同优化方法。

Method: 提出分层优化框架：上层使用无求解器贪婪启发式算法（SFGH）实现车辆实时调度（非抢占式服务、区间顺序分配）；下层采用深度强化学习（DRL）优化电池/氢罐运行及光伏分配，以最小化运行成本。

Result: 案例研究及对比、消融、泛化分析表明所提框架有效且鲁棒；习得的调度策略在不同交通需求场景下无需重新训练即可保持强性能。

Conclusion: 该分层框架能够有效解耦车辆调度与能源调度，实现实时运行优化，且具有良好的泛化能力，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Integrated electric-hydrogen infrastructures are becoming increasingly important with the growing deployment of electric vehicles (EVs) and hydrogen vehicles (HVs) in transport systems. However, the strong coupling between vehicle scheduling and multi-energy dispatch introduces significant operational challenges. This paper models an integrated electric-hydrogen-transport system (EHTS) and proposes a hierarchical optimisation framework that couples vehicle scheduling and downstream energy dispatch through a sequential, demand-driven two-layer structure.
  In the vehicle scheduling layer, a solver-free greedy heuristic (SFGH) algorithm is developed to avoid repeated optimisation solving, enabling real-time EV charging and HV refuelling under non-preemptive service and within-interval sequential assignment. The resulting charging and refuelling demands are subsequently passed to the energy dispatch layer, where a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based approach is designed to optimise battery operation, hydrogen-tank operation, and PV generation allocation to minimise the overall operational cost of the EHTS while satisfying the scheduled transport demand.
  Representative case studies, together with comparative, ablation, and generalisation analyses, demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework. Furthermore, the learned dispatch policy maintains strong performance across diverse transport-demand scenarios without retraining, demonstrating robust generalisation capability for practical deployment.

</details>


### [170] [Horizon Selection in Physics-Enhanced Neural ODEs: Theoretical Insights and Flux Linkage Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25804)
*Giulio Montecchio,Benjamin Hartmann,Sven Reimann,Maximilian Manderla,Jan Achterhold,Daniel Görges*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理增强神经常微分方程训练中的积分视界问题，提出通过延长视界来减少物理参数估计偏差、提取数据残差信息并提升泛化能力，以永磁同步电机为案例证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究物理增强神经常微分方程中训练积分视界的关键作用，解决传统短视界训练导致物理参数估计偏差和泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 基于经典非线性系统辨识理论分析视界扩展的作用，提出延长训练视界的框架：利用长视界减少物理参数估计偏差、从数据中提取残差信息、并作为正则化项提升模型泛化能力。

Result: 在永磁同步电机建模任务中，该方法成功实现了磁链图和电阻的联合估计，验证了长视界训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 延长训练积分视界能有效改善物理增强神经常微分方程中参数估计的偏差问题，同时提升模型的泛化性能，为物理信息神经网络在动力学系统中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: The integration horizon during the training plays a critical role in Physics-Enhanced Neural Ordinary Differential Equations. We draw conclusions about horizon extension in the training of Neural Ordinary Differential Equations based on classical nonlinear system identification of input-output models. In light of this insight, we propose a framework that exploits longer horizons to reduce bias in physical parameter estimates, extracts residual information from data, and acts as a regularizer improving generalization. In the learning of a model for permanent magnet synchronous machine, the method is used to jointly estimate the flux map and the resistance.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Parallel Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization via Rank-Two KKT Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24813)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反射策略的模型信赖域方法，利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式将KKT矩阵的更新复杂度从O(m³)降至O(n²)，并嵌入主从并行TCG求解器，在530个基准问题上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型信赖域方法构造二次插值模型时需要求解KKT系统，计算复杂度为O(m³)，限制了并行可扩展性。本文旨在降低计算开销并提升并行效率。

Method: 利用最小Frobenius范数更新模型中KKT矩阵的特性——依赖于坐标内积，单轴反射仅改变KKT矩阵的一行一列，产生秩至多2的扰动，通过Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式实现O(n²)的逆更新；反射在中心欧几里得信赖域中为等距变换，保持插值点集的poisedness常数；结合主从并行架构与截断共轭梯度子问题求解器。

Result: 在530个基准问题上进行数值实验，与已有的DFO求解器进行性能对比。

Conclusion: 该反射机制在标准fully linear模型管理假设下支持一阶全局收敛，显著降低了计算复杂度并提升了并行可扩展性。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses unconstrained problems $\min_{\x\in\RR^n} f(\x)$ where $f$ is accessed only through a zeroth-order oracle. Model-based trust-region methods construct underdetermined quadratic interpolation models from $\mathcal{O}(n)$ points and solve a KKT system to determine model parameters, costing $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ operations and limiting parallel scalability. It is shown that the KKT matrix for the minimum Frobenius norm updating model depends entirely on inner products of shifted coordinates. Reflecting the interpolation set across a single coordinate axis preserves these inner products and changes only one row and column of the KKT matrix, inducing a rank-at-most-two perturbation whose inverse update via the Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury formula costs $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ when $m=\mathcal{O}(n)$. The reflection is an isometry in centered Euclidean trust regions and preserves the poisedness constant of the interpolation set; together with standard fully linear model-management assumptions this supports first-order global convergence. The mechanism is embedded in a master-worker parallel algorithm with a Truncated Conjugate Gradient subproblem solver. Numerical results on 530 benchmark problems compare performance against an established DFO solver.

</details>


### [172] [Extremal problem of area approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24828)
*O. O. Pokutnyi,R. R. Salimov,M. V. Stefanchuk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑曲线的最优分段线性逼近问题，以最小化原函数图与插值折线间的包围区域面积。针对二次抛物线，给出最小面积的精确值并证明最优划分为均匀划分；针对三次函数，证明最优插值节点非均匀分布，并给出两个内点时的精确坐标与最小面积。结果可应用于CAD系统的刀具路径优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题来源于激光/等离子切割、铣削、增材制造等工艺中数控设备控制程序的制备，在这些场景中需要以分段线性路径逼近理想曲线，而最小化偏差面积有助于提高加工精度和效率。

Method: 从数学上建立了曲线最优分段线性逼近的优化模型，以原函数与插值折线间封闭区域面积为目标函数。针对二次抛物线（凸函数）和三次函数（非凸）分别进行理论分析，求解最优插值节点位置及对应的最小面积。

Result: 对于二次抛物线，最小面积可精确求出，且最优区间划分为均匀划分；对于三次函数，最优插值节点非均匀分布；给出了两个内点情形下节点精确坐标与最小面积的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 研究为CAD系统中基于最小偏差面积准则的刀具路径优化提供了理论依据，不同函数类型的最优节点分布特征对实际工程应用具有指导意义。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of optimal piecewise linear approximation of a smooth curve, in which the area of the region enclosed between the graph of the original function and the constructed interpolating polyline is minimized. This problem arises in technological processes such as laser and plasma cutting of metals, milling, additive manufacturing, and in the preparation of control programs for CNC equipment. For a quadratic parabola, the exact value of the minimum area is established, and it is shown that the optimal partition of the interval is uniform. For a cubic function, it is demonstrated that the optimal interpolation nodes are distributed non-uniformly; in the case of two internal partition points, their exact coordinates and the corresponding minimum area are obtained. The results can be applied in CAD systems for optimizing tool paths based on the criterion of minimum deviation area.

</details>


### [173] [Functional H_infinity Filtering for Descriptor Systems with Incrementally Quadratic Nonlinearities under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性奇异系统提出了一种可直接初始化的显式状态空间$H_\infty$滤波器，通过增量二次约束刻画非线性特性，采用秩条件与LMI保证误差动态渐近稳定并抑制外部扰动，滤波器阶数不超过待估计函数向量维数，有效降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统$H_\infty$滤波方法对奇异系统施加了过于严格的正则性假设，且采用隐式描述形式的滤波器，实际实现困难；同时现有方法主要针对全阶或降阶状态估计，当仅需估计状态的某个特定函数时计算效率低下。

Method: 1) 直接在显式状态空间框架下设计滤波器，允许任意初始值；2) 滤波器阶数不超过待估计函数向量维数以降低计算复杂度；3) 采用增量二次约束（IQC）及适当乘子矩阵刻画非线性（涵盖Lipschitz、单边Lipschitz、单调等非线性）；4) 通过系统矩阵的秩条件结合一组线性矩阵不等式（LMI）建立滤波器存在性充分判据。

Result: 建立了保证估计误差动态渐近稳定且外部扰动对误差影响满足预设$L_2$性能界的存在性条件（秩条件+LMI），数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法突破了传统方法的正则性限制和隐式滤波器实现困难，以更低的计算代价实现了非线性奇异系统的函数$H_\infty$滤波。

Abstract: This paper develops a functional H_infinity filter for nonlinear descriptor systems subject to external disturbances. Conventional H_infinity filtering approaches for descriptor systems impose restrictive regularity assumptions and employ implicit descriptor-form filters, leading to practical implementation difficulties. Moreover, existing approaches mainly target full-or reduced-order state estimation, which is computationally inefficient when only a specific functional of the state is required. To address these limitations, the filter is formulated directly in an explicit state-space framework and can be initialized with arbitrary initial values. The filter order is chosen to be less than or equal to the dimension of the functional vector to be estimated, thereby reducing computational complexity. The considered nonlinearities are characterized using incremental quadratic constraints parameterized by appropriate multiplier matrices, which encompass Lipschitz, one-sided Lipschitz, monotone, and many other nonlinearities. Sufficient criteria for the existence of the proposed filter are established through a rank condition imposed on the system matrices together with a set of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs). Under these conditions, asymptotic stability of the estimation error dynamics is guaranteed, while the influence of external disturbances on the error is bounded within a prescribed L2-performance framework. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate and validate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [174] [Derivative-free optimization approach for structured symmetric matrices with fixed eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25046)
*Carmo P. Brás,Evelin H. M. Krulikovski,Marcos Raydan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于无导数优化（DFO）的模型，用于求解一类特殊的逆对称稀疏矩阵特征值问题，其中部分矩阵元素已固定，部分元素需非零但值未知，其余元素完全自由。该模型通过全局定向直接搜索（GLODS）等确定性DFO方法求解，并探讨了多种启发式策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决逆结构化对称矩阵特征值问题，这类问题在图论和稀疏矩阵应用中具有重要实际意义，但传统方法难以处理非光滑目标函数。

Method: 将逆特征值问题建模为无导数优化（DFO）问题，目标函数通过计算对称矩阵特征值来评估（非可微）。采用确定性DFO方法——全局定向直接搜索（GLODS），并辅以多种启发式策略，基于Lipschitz连续性讨论收敛性。

Result: 初步数值实验展示了确定性GLODS方法与多种启发式策略在不同场景下的性能对比，验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的DFO模型能有效求解稀疏模式逆特征值问题，GLODS方法具有理论收敛保证，启发式策略在某些场景下可进一步提升求解效率。

Abstract: A Derivative-Free Optimization (DFO) model is developed and analyzed for solving inverse structured symmetric matrix problems for which the eigenvalues are specified. Some (zero and nonzero) entries are preassigned and cannot be changed, while others should be nonzero but their values are not given. The rest of the entries are completely free. The obtained matrix must meet these criteria and have the specified eigenvalues. This specialized inverse eigenvalue problem is relevant to various applications and is linked to determining the graph, with weights on the undirected edges, of the matrix associated with its sparse pattern. Our novel optimization model requires computing the eigenvalues of a symmetric matrix to evaluate the non-differentiable objective function. We apply deterministic DFO schemes, specifically the global variant GLODS of the well-known family of directional direct search (DDS) methods. We discuss its convergence properties which are based on the fact that the objective function of our model is Lipschitz continuous. Additionally, we explore the potential benefits of using several well-established heuristic strategies to solve the proposed optimization model. We present preliminary numerical results to illustrate and compare the performance of the considered deterministic and heuristic DFO options in various possible scenarios.

</details>


### [175] [Functional Subspace Projection for Detection of Coordinated Stealthy Attacks in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25093)
*Koto Omiloli,Satish Vedula,Olugbenga Moses Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多区域电力系统中协同动态虚假数据注入攻击(FDIAs)的建模与检测联合框架，通过核嵌入函数子空间检测(KEFSD)方法在再生核希尔伯特空间中建模残差轨迹，有效识别异常时间模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法大多依赖瞬时测量值，无法有效检测可在每个时间步保持隐蔽的协同动态攻击，亟需能够捕捉攻击时间演化特征和跨区域协调特性的检测方法。

Method: 1) 提出时间聚合攻击模型描述攻击的时序演化和区域间协调；2) 提出KEFSD方法，在RKHS中建模残差轨迹并利用RKHS约束的函数PCA识别异常时间模式。

Result: 在修改的IEEE 14节点系统上，KEFSD相比传统基于残差的2-范数检测器取得了更优的检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合框架有效提升了多区域电力系统对协同动态FDIAs的检测能力。

Abstract: Modern power grids are increasingly vulnerable to coordinated cyber-attacks, particularly false data injection attacks (FDIAs) that can evade conventional residual-based detectors. While most existing detection methods rely on instantaneous measurements, coordinated dynamic attacks can remain stealthy at each time step while introducing structured temporal deviations. This paper develops a joint framework for modeling and detecting such attacks in multi-area power systems. A time-aggregated attack model is first formulated to capture temporal evolution and inter-area coordination. For detection, a kernel-embedded functional subspace detection (KEFSD) method is proposed, which models residual trajectories in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) and employs RKHS-constrained functional principal component analysis (PCA) to identify anomalous temporal patterns. Simulation results on a modified IEEE 14-bus system demonstrates the proposed method achieves improved detection performance compared to the conventional residual based 2-norm detector.

</details>


### [176] [Intercity EV Charging Readiness in Türkiye: A Countrywide Scheduling and Economic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25143)
*Taner Cokyasar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文评估了土耳其电动汽车充电基础设施支持城际旅行的准备情况，通过优化超200万次合成城际行程，发现电动汽车在运营成本上具有23-26%的优势，TCO优势为10-12%，但基础设施投资回报高度依赖充电站规模（5插头为正收益，50插头为负收益），其中车辆放电率是主导经济杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏针对新兴市场（如土耳其）全国范围内、车辆级别的城际电动汽车充电准备情况的系统性评估，特别是结合充电基础设施布局、经济性分析与政策洞察的研究。

Method: 采用基于有向无环图的调度框架，优化超200万次合成城际EV行程；评估9,641个充电站；设计8种场景（功率5-350kW、站容量5/50插头）及200次参数敏感性分析（续航、放电率、充电功率、SoC边界）；三层经济分析（运营成本、TCO、基础设施投资）。

Result: EV运营成本优势23-26%（得益于土耳其燃料与电力价格比），TCO优势10-12%；站容量5插头时基础设施投资正收益，50插头时负收益；车辆放电率是最关键的经济变量。
