Conclusion: 该研究是首个针对新兴市场的全国性、车辆级城际EV充电准备度评估，为政策制定者提供了基础设施缺口、充电站规模权衡及采用激励措施的可操作见解。

Abstract: This paper assesses the readiness of Türkiye's electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure to support cost-competitive intercity travel compared to internal combustion engine (ICE) vehicles. Utilizing a directed acyclic graph-based scheduling framework, charging stops are optimized for over 2 million synthetic intercity EV trips across the national highway network, evaluating 9,641 licensed charging stations. An eight-scenario case study examines the interplay between charger power (5--350 kW) and station capacity (5 and 50 plugs), supplemented by a 200-run parametric sensitivity analysis on EV range, discharge rate, charger power, and state-of-charge (SoC) operating bounds. A three-tier economic analysis isolates operational costs, total cost of ownership (TCO), and infrastructure investments. Economically, EVs demonstrate a 23--26% operational cost advantage driven by Türkiye's favorable fuel-to-electricity price ratio, maintaining a 10--12% TCO advantage despite higher purchase prices. However, full-system infrastructure savings heavily depend on station sizing, yielding positive returns at 5 plugs per station but negative returns at 50. Ultimately, vehicle discharge rate emerges as the dominant economic lever. This study provides the first countrywide, vehicle-level assessment of intercity EV charging readiness for an emerging market, offering actionable insights for policymakers on infrastructure gaps, station sizing tradeoffs, and adoption incentives.

</details>


### [177] [A Foundational Perspective for Partitional Clustering on Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25144)
*Derya Ipek Eroglu,Cem Iyigun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对网络上的划分聚类进行了理论分析，比较了硬分配（P-Median、Sum of Squares Clustering）和软分配（Probabilistic Distance Clustering、Fuzzy C-Means）四种模型，发现聚类中心可位于顶点或边上，其中SSC和FCM可产生边上的最优中心，而PMP和PDC则倾向于顶点放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络聚类研究多局限于顶点作为聚类中心，缺乏对聚类中心可位于边上的理论分析，且对不同目标函数下硬分配与软分配模型的结构性差异理解不足。

Method: 通过数学分析，研究四种聚类模型（PMP、SSC、PDC、FCM）在网络上（顶点和边均可作为聚类中心）的结构性质，重点分析分配瓶颈点、顶点受限解等概念对最优聚类中心位置的影响。

Result: SSC和FCM可产生位于边上的最优聚类中心，而PMP和PDC则固有地偏好顶点放置；发现了分配瓶颈点等结构性属性在不同模型中的差异化表现。

Conclusion: 这些理论见解为设计高效聚类算法提供了新方向，对设施选址、网络设计以及基于嵌入图的相似性搜索等现代检索系统具有实际意义。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical analysis of partitional clustering on networks, analyzing both hard and soft assignment schemes with different objective functions. Cluster centers are not restricted to vertices but can also be located along the edges. We examine four key models: P-Median (PMP) and Sum of Squares Clustering (SSC) under hard assignment, and Probabilistic Distance Clustering (PDC) and Fuzzy C-Means (FCM) under soft assignment. Through mathematical analysis, we uncover structural properties that differentiate these models, such as the significance of assignment bottleneck points and the role of vertex-restricted solutions in determining optimal cluster centers. Our findings reveal that, while SSC and FCM can yield optimal centers along edges, PMP and PDC inherently favor vertex placement, leading to insights into clustering behavior on networks. These insights offer new directions for designing efficient algorithms and have implications ranging from facility location and network design to clustering on the embedding graphs that power similarity search in modern retrieval systems.

</details>


### [178] [Distributed Nonlinear Equality-Constrained Optimization via Feedback Linearization and Singular Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25193)
*Zihao Ren,Lei Wang,Hongye Su,Guodong Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸目标与非线性等式约束的分布式优化问题，提出了一种基于反馈线性化与奇异摄动的框架。该框架通过分离可行性调控与流形优化，并引入快速残差跟踪动力学实现分布式实施，在局部二次增长条件下证明了指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法在处理非凸目标与非线性等式约束时面临挑战：可行性是网络耦合的，而大多数指数收敛方法依赖于凸性或仿射约束。本文旨在解决这一关键空白。

Method: 提出反馈线性化与奇异摄动框架：1) 构造理想反馈线性化动力学，其输出行为可显式指定，零输出动力学对应于可行流形上的投影梯度流；2) 针对非局部代数方程不可分布式实施的问题，引入快速残差跟踪动力学替代，形成奇异摄动实现；3) 采用显式欧拉离散化保持指数收敛性。

Result: 1) 在局部二次增长条件下，证明理想动力学与分布式动力学均局部指数收敛；2) 时间尺度分离足够强时，分布式实现的收敛速率可任意接近理想动力学；3) 显式欧拉离散化保持局部指数收敛；4) 在更严格流形正则条件下，吸引域可扩展至可行流形的管状邻域，对仿射局部等式约束可扩展至整个可行状态空间。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了非凸非线性等式约束下的分布式优化难题，实现了仅依赖局部与邻域信息的指数收敛算法，并具有良好的离散化保持特性。

Abstract: Distributed optimization becomes particularly challenging when nonconvex objectives are combined with nonlinear equality constraints: feasibility is network coupled, while most existing exponentially convergent methods rely on convexity or affine constraints. This paper introduces a feedback-linearization and singular-perturbation framework for two representative problem classes involving, respectively, distributed local nonlinear equality constraints and nonlinear aggregate equality constraints. The framework separates the regulation of consensus and feasibility residuals from optimization along the feasible manifold. Specifically, an ideal feedback-linearized dynamics is constructed whose output behavior can be explicitly assigned, while its zero-output dynamics coincides with the projected gradient flow of the aggregate objective on the feasible manifold. The ideal feedback-linearizing input, however, is determined by a state-dependent, network-coupled algebraic equation and is therefore not directly implementable in a distributed manner. To overcome this obstruction, we replace the nonlocal algebraic solution with a fast residual-tracking dynamics, yielding a singular-perturbation realization that uses only local and neighboring information. Under a local quadratic-growth condition imposed only on the feasible manifold, we establish local exponential convergence of both the ideal and distributed dynamics. For sufficiently strong time-scale separation, the convergence rate of the distributed realization can be chosen arbitrarily close to that of the ideal dynamics. Explicit Euler discretizations are also proved to preserve local exponential convergence. Under stronger manifold regularity conditions, the region of attraction extends to a tubular neighborhood of the feasible manifold and, for affine local equality constraints, to the whole admissible state space.

</details>


### [179] [Projection and contraction methods with double inertial steps for variational inclusion problems on Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25203)
*Moin Uddin,Mohammed Alshahrani,Qamrul Hasan Ansari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对希尔伯特空间中的变分包含问题，提出了三种融合双惯性技术的投影-收缩算法，分别实现弱收敛、R-线性收敛和强收敛（到最小范数解），无需先验Lipschitz常数，并在分裂可行问题和弹性网络正则化问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影-收缩算法求解变分包含问题时，收敛速度慢且依赖Lipschitz常数的先验知识，缺乏同时兼顾惯性加速、自适应步长和强收敛性的统一框架。作者旨在设计三种算法分别满足不同收敛需求。

Method: (1) 算法O：单值算子单调且Lipschitz连续下，采用双惯性项+自适应步长的投影-收缩迭代实现弱收敛；(2) 算法T：在强单调假设下对算法O进行修正，实现R-线性收敛；(3) 算法DIM：无需强单调性，通过额外正则化步骤获得强收敛到最小范数解。

Result: (1) 算法O在单调+Lipschitz条件下弱收敛；(2) 算法T在强单调条件下R-线性收敛（数值实验验证）；(3) 算法DIM无需强单调性即可强收敛到最小范数解。三者均在分裂可行问题和弹性网络正则化问题上优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 双惯性投影-收缩框架有效平衡了算法收敛速度与条件要求，提供了从弱收敛到强收敛的完整算法族，且自适应步长设计降低了实际应用中的调参成本。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose three projection--contraction algorithms for solving variational inclusion problems in the setting of Hilbert spaces, each incorporating a double inertial technique into the projection--contraction framework. The first algorithm \algoO achieves weak convergence under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the single-valued operator, with an adaptive stepsize rule that does not require prior knowledge of the Lipschitz constant. A modified variant \algoT of \algoO attains $R$-linear convergence under the strong monotonicity assumption. The third algorithm \algoDIM obtains strong convergence to the minimum-norm solution without requiring strong monotonicity. We illustrate the proposed methods on an abstract variational inclusion problem and apply them to the split feasibility problem and the elastic net regularization problem, comparing them with existing methods in the literature. The $R$-linear convergence of \algoT is also verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [180] [Event-Triggered Discrete-Time Multivariable Extremum Seeking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25223)
*Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstić,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离散时间事件触发极值搜索框架，用于多变量非线性系统的实时优化。与传统周期性更新不同，该方法仅在满足状态依赖触发条件时更新控制动作，实现非周期执行，大幅减少驱动和通信开销。通过离散时间平均分析与李雅普诺夫方法，证明了系统轨迹能实际收敛到未知极值点附近，且平均动力学具有指数稳定性。数值仿真表明，该方法在显著减少触发事件的同时，能保持与经典方法相当的优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间极值搜索方法依赖周期性输入更新，在资源受限的数字和网络化控制系统中造成不必要的驱动与通信开销。如何在保证极值搜索优化性能的前提下，大幅减少控制更新次数，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出事件触发的离散时间极值搜索框架，设计状态依赖的触发条件，仅在必要时更新控制输入。结合离散时间平均分析与李雅普诺夫方法，证明系统轨迹实际收敛到未知极值邻域，并建立平均动力学的指数稳定性。

Result: 理论证明所提事件触发机制能保持经典极值搜索的核心优化机理；数值仿真表明在显著减少驱动事件次数的前提下，优化性能与周期性方法相当。

Conclusion: 该框架成功调和了极值搜索所需的周期性激励与事件触发的非周期性执行之间的结构性矛盾，适用于资源感知的数字和网络化控制实现。

Abstract: This paper introduces a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking framework for real-time optimization of multivariable nonlinear systems. In contrast to conventional discrete-time extremum seeking implementations that rely on periodic input updates, the proposed scheme updates the control action only when a state-dependent triggering condition is met, enabling aperiodic execution and substantial reduction of actuation and communication effort. The resulting closed-loop architecture reconciles two structurally different paradigms: the periodic excitation required for gradient recovery in extremum seeking and the aperiodic philosophy of event-triggered control. By combining discrete-time averaging arguments with Lyapunov-based analysis, we prove practical convergence of the system trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum and exponential stability of the corresponding average dynamics despite the loss of uniform sampling. The results show that appropriately designed triggering rules preserve the essential optimization mechanisms of classical extremum seeking while drastically reducing the number of input updates. Numerical simulations demonstrate that comparable optimization performance can be achieved with significantly fewer actuation events, highlighting the suitability of the method for resource-aware digital and networked control implementations.

</details>


### [181] [Proximal Gradient Methods for Unconstrained Set Optimization Problems with Set-Valued Maps of Finite Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25252)
*Ravi Raushan,Debdas Ghosh,Anshika,V. Vetrivel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种近端梯度方法（带线搜索与不带线搜索）用于求解无约束集值优化问题，在凸、尖、闭的固体锥诱导的下集序关系下建立了全局收敛性，并证明了$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$的收敛速率，通过数值实验验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集值优化问题的求解方法（如最速下降法）收敛性结果有限，亟需发展具有更强收敛保证和更快收敛速率的优化算法。

Method: 提出了两种近端梯度方法（带线搜索与不带线搜索）；利用弱有效点族刻画弱极小点；建立平稳性条件及与弱极小点的联系；基于平稳性条件定义非平稳点处的下降方向；设计Armijo型线搜索条件并证明步长存在性。

Result: 两种方法在温和假设下均实现了全局收敛；带线搜索方法的收敛分析超越了此前最速下降法的结果；两种方法均达到$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$的收敛速率；数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的近端梯度方法为求解集值优化问题提供了理论更完善、收敛速率更优的求解框架，在收敛性分析上取得了对传统方法的理论推进。

Abstract: This work presents two different types of proximal gradient methods, with line search and without line search, for solving unconstrained set-valued optimization problems under the lower set-less ordering relation induced by a solid cone that is convex, pointed, and closed. The objective mapping of the problem involves finitely many functions, with each one being the sum of a continuously differentiable function and a convex function that is proper and closed. We present an approach to characterize weakly minimal points of the problem with the help of weakly efficient points of a family of vector optimization problems. Thereafter, we establish a stationarity condition along with its connection with weakly minimal points of the problem under study. Based on the stationary condition, the concept of a descent direction at a non-stationary point is discussed. In view of the line search-based method, we formulate an Armijo-type line search condition and establish the existence of such a step-size. For the proposed methods, global convergence is established under mild assumptions. The convergence analysis of the proximal gradient method with line search provides a theoretical advancement over the convergence results previously established for the steepest descent method in set-valued optimization problems. In addition, we analyze the computational complexity of the proposed methods and show that both methods achieve a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$. Numerical results are reported to test the performance of the methods in practice.

</details>


### [182] [Microsecond-Class Powered-Descent Optimization via Exact Condensation and Strong Convex Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25260)
*Wenbo Li,Ziqi Xu,Dai Shen,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对燃料主导的动力下降凸优化问题，提出了三种结构简化方法：引入低权重能量项使控制解唯一、终端状态灵敏度递归消除中间状态、利用燃料范数与推力球的共享径向结构导出闭式近端算子。最终将300个原始变量和174个等式乘子的30节点基线问题压缩为90个控制变量和6个终端乘子，在C17数组上以固定预算外推比例-积分投影梯度迭代求解，在火星动力下降案例中迭代次数从2744降至93，燃料指标仅增加0.033%，平均求解时间68.2微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的燃料最优动力下降凸优化问题存在状态变量多、燃料epigraph变量多、动力学等式约束多等计算负担，且纯燃料目标函数缺乏强凸性导致解不唯一、收敛慢。亟需一种结构简化方法实现快速实时求解。

Method: 三种结构简化：(1)引入维度一致的低权重能量项使解唯一；(2)终端状态灵敏度递归精确消除所有中间状态，结合可逆行归一化将问题从300个原始变量+174个等式乘子压缩为90个控制变量+6个终端乘子；(3)利用燃料范数与推力球的共享径向结构导出由组收缩和幅度裁剪组成的精确闭式近端算子。最后采用固定预算外推比例-积分投影梯度迭代在C17数组上求解。

Result: 在火星动力下降案例中（能量权重0.02，相对参考解容差10^{-3}），迭代次数从纯燃料全状态epigraph基线的2744次降至93次，燃料指标仅增加0.033%。平均端到端求解时间68.2微秒，P99为128.1微秒（Intel i7-10875H）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于凸核心问题的快速求解而非新的无损凸化定理，通过结构简化和定制化算法显著降低了计算复杂度，实现了微秒级实时求解，适用于计算资源受限的在线制导场景。

Abstract: Fuel-dominant powered descent can be written as a convex program, but the usual full-state epigraph formulation still carries many state variables, fuel epigraph variables, and dynamics equalities. In addition, the pure-fuel objective provides no strong-convexity curvature. This paper combines three structural reductions. First, a dimensionally consistent low-weight energy term makes the control solution unique. Second, a terminal-state sensitivity recursion eliminates every intermediate state exactly; invertible row normalization turns the 30-node baseline with 300 primal variables and 174 equality multipliers into a problem with 90 control variables and six terminal multipliers. Third, the shared radial structure of the fuel norm and thrust ball gives an exact closed-form proximal operator consisting of group shrinkage followed by magnitude clipping. The condensed problem is solved with a fixed-budget extrapolated proportional--integral projected-gradient iteration implemented in fixed-size C17 arrays. In a Mars powered-descent case with energy weight 0.02 and relative reference-solution tolerance $10^{-3}$, the iteration count decreases from 2744 for the pure-fuel full-state epigraph baseline to 93, while the fuel metric increases by only 0.033\%. The mean end-to-end solve time is \SI{68.2}{\micro\second}, and P99 is \SI{128.1}{\micro\second}, on an Intel i7-10875H. Because the thrust set in this test case is already a convex ball, the contribution is fast solution of the convex core rather than a new lossless-convexification theorem.

</details>


### [183] [A Numerical Investigation of the Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn Inequality for Polyhedral Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25419)
*Josué D. Díaz-Avalos,Antoine Laurain*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维凸多面体中如何最小化第一Dirichlet特征值的问题，提出了一种基于支撑超平面参数化和拉格朗日框架的数值方法，发现最优形状包括柏拉图立体、棱柱、十四面体以及具有对称性的非规则多面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn不等式推广到多面体约束情形，探究在固定面数条件下，什么样的凸多面体形状能使Laplace算子的第一特征值最小。

Method: 通过支撑超平面对多面体进行参数化，将原问题转化为等价的有限维约束最小化问题；采用拉格朗日框架进行数值求解，结合有限维扰动和无穷维形状导数进行灵敏度分析。

Result: 数值结果得到了已知的最优多面体（如柏拉图立体、棱柱、十四面体），同时也发现了具有多种对称性的非规则最优形状。

Conclusion: 提出的数值方法有效解决了固定面数下多面体特征值优化问题，揭示了最优形状的多样性，为后续形状优化研究提供了数值依据。

Abstract: We present numerical results for the problem of minimizing the first Dirichlet eigenvalue of the Laplacian among three-dimensional convex polyhedra with a prescribed number of facets. This problem can be viewed as a polyhedral version of the classical Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn inequality. Using a parameterization of polytopes by supporting hyperplanes, the problem is first reformulated as an equivalent finite-dimensional constrained minimization problem. A Lagrangian framework is employed for the numerical solution. Under the assumption that each vertex is incident to exactly $d$ facets, the sensitivity analysis of the Lagrangian combines finite-dimensional perturbations of the facets of $d$-dimensional polyhedra with infinite-dimensional shape derivatives of both the eigenvalue and the volume. Depending on the number of facets, the numerical results yield well-known polyhedra, such as Platonic solids, prisms, and the tetrakaidecahedron, or reveal nonregular optimal shapes exhibiting several symmetries.

</details>


### [184] [Exact discrete-adjoint optimization of trap timing and placement in a Stieltjes-time reaction-diffusion model: A Galicia case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25450)
*Francisco J. Fernández,Iván Area*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为季节性种群模型提出了一个有限维的定时与放置控制问题，基于Stieltjes时间反应-扩散模型，推导了完全离散残差的精确伴随。通过有限元与隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式处理传播、区室置换、重置和历史相位平均。数值实验验证了机器精度级别的梯度一致性，伴随方法加速比达7.5-30.9倍。在真实加利西亚网格上对黄腿胡蜂(Vespa velutina)诱捕行动进行应用，最终双簇控制方案可行，梯度范数为2.97×10^{-5}，在最高分辨率下联合控制相比仅时间控制降低诊断目标0.49209%。框架完全可复现，但不声称全局最优性或校准的田间管理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性种群模型需同时结合扩散、非活跃期和突发生物转移，同时要保持对局部干预的可微性，以便进行优化控制。现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 将问题形式化为Stieltjes时间反应-扩散模型上的有限维定时与放置控制问题；采用连续有限元与隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式离散化；推导完全离散残差的精确伴随；通过直接灵敏度与伴随梯度的等价性验证可微性。

Result: 机器精度梯度一致性（低于3×10^{-15}）；伴随加速比7.5-30.9倍；真实网格上双簇控制梯度范数2.97×10^{-5}；细化比0.4984；联合控制降低目标0.49209%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效求解季节性种群模型的控制问题，伴随方法显著提升计算效率；实际应用显示联合控制优于仅时间控制；但结果依赖于未校准参数，不直接反映实际诱捕效果，且不保证全局最优性。

Abstract: Seasonal population models must combine diffusion, inactive periods and abrupt biological transfers while remaining differentiable with respect to localized interventions. We formulate a finite-dimensional timing-and-placement control problem for a Stieltjes-time reaction--diffusion model and derive the exact adjoint of the fully discrete residual. Continuous finite elements and an implicit Stieltjes--Euler scheme represent propagation, compartment replacement, resets and historical phase averages. For the resulting box-constrained penalized problem, we prove well-posedness and differentiability of the discrete control-to-state map, existence of a minimizer, first-order stationarity, and equivalence between direct-sensitivity and adjoint gradients. Benchmark tests show machine-precision agreement (below $3\times10^{-15}$) and adjoint speed-ups from about $7.5$ to $30.9$ as the number of traps increases from one to four. The method is applied to \textit{Vespa velutina} trap campaigns on a realistic Galicia mesh. Smoothed activation windows, domain-normalized moving kernels and the complete adjoint are independently verified. The final two-cluster control remains admissible and has a scaled gradient infinity norm of $2.97\times10^{-5}$ with 120 temporal subdivisions per month. Fixed-control evaluations at 60, 120 and 240 subdivisions yield a refinement-increment ratio of $0.4984$. At the finest level, the joint control outperforms time-only and reference controls, reducing the diagnostic objective by $0.49209%$. This reduction depends on uncalibrated trap-to-mortality intensities and should not be interpreted as field capture efficacy. The framework is fully reproducible, without claiming global optimality or a calibrated field-management prescription.

</details>


### [185] [Optimal Parameter Design for DIGing on Minimizing Unweighted Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25463)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过控制理论中的Routh稳定性判据，为分布式不精确梯度追踪（DIGing）算法推导了最优最坏情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数设计方法，并指出即使对于最简单的目标函数，DIGing收敛速度也较慢，因此需要加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化算法中缺乏设计合适参数以实现更快收敛的通用方法。

Method: 将DIGing迭代算法表示为动态线性系统，分解为不同图频率得到解耦子系统；利用控制理论的Routh稳定性判据推导最优最坏情况收敛率及对应参数的显式公式。

Result: 得到了最优最坏情况收敛率的显式公式及对应参数；发现即使对于最简单的目标函数（未加权平方和），DIGing收敛速度仍然缓慢。

Conclusion: 所提方法可作为DIGing算法在求解一般目标函数时进行最优参数设计的第一步，且加速是必要的。

Abstract: There is no general method for designing proper parameters to achieve faster convergence in distributed optimization algorithms. In this paper, we consider the distributed inexact gradient tracking (DIGing) algorithm with the objective function being the unweighted sum of squares. By representing the iteration algorithm as a dynamical linear system, we decompose it into different graph frequencies and obtain a set of decoupled subsystems, on which we can easily analyze the convergence rate. By using Routh stability criterion from control theory, we derive the explicit formula of the optimal worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. We can see that the convergence rate of DIGing is slow even for the simplest objective functions, thus acceleration is necessary for general application. The proposed method can be viewed as the first step toward optimal parameter design of DIGing algorithm in solving general objective functions.

</details>


### [186] [Conditional gradient methods on unbounded feasible regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25594)
*R. Díaz Millán,Tuan Thanh Lu,Julien Ugon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简洁的紧致约束方法，使得条件梯度法（无需投影）能够应用于无界可行域，并保持标准收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典条件梯度法的收敛理论要求可行域是紧致的，但许多自然凸可行域（如线性约束）是无界闭集。现有扩展方法要么修改算法本身，要么需要额外假设，作者希望在不改变算法核心的前提下，通过简单的紧致约束技巧来解决此问题。

Method: 提出两种紧致约束方案：(1) 固定约束：预先选取一个包含初始目标子水平集的紧凸集；(2) 动态约束：在迭代过程中通过交集生成紧凸集，不断更新约束。两者均仅在紧子集上求解线性优化，但保证收敛到原始问题的全局最优。

Result: 对于光滑凸目标函数，两种方案均能保持标准超线性收敛率。对于非光滑情形，基于曲率假设给出了次线性收敛率的次梯度扩展。数值实验验证了固定和动态约束在无界可行域上的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过简单的紧致约束技巧，条件梯度法可自然地推广到无界可行域，无需修改算法核心，且收敛理论可以保持。这一结果为条件梯度法在更广泛实际问题（如线性规划、半定规划）中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The conditional gradient method is attractive when linear minimization over the feasible region is substantially cheaper than projection. Its classical convergence theory, however, is formulated for compact feasible sets, whereas many natural convex feasible regions are closed and unbounded. This paper studies a simple compact-restriction principle for applying conditional gradient steps to unbounded feasible regions. The first scheme uses one compact convex set containing the initial objective sublevel set. The second scheme updates the restriction by intersecting compact convex sets generated along the iterations. In both cases the linear minimization oracle is solved only over compact subsets, but the resulting objective values converge to the global optimum of the original problem, provided the compact restrictions contain the corresponding objective sublevel sets. For smooth convex objectives we obtain the standard superlinear convergence rate of the objective. We also record constructive restrictions based on strong convexity, exact sublevel sets, and epigraph caps, and include a nonsmooth conditional subgradient extension with a sublinear convergence rate under a curvature assumption. Numerical experiments illustrate the behaviour of the fixed and dynamic restrictions on unbounded feasible regions.

</details>


### [187] [Bilinear Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Unknown Nonlinear Systems via Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25658)
*Yuki Higuchi,Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于Koopman算子的数据驱动模型预测控制中，双线性Koopman实现存在建模误差且受限的鲁棒控制问题，提出了一种新的鲁棒MPC框架，通过将预测状态重新投影回原始流形来构建误差感知的离散时间预测器，并基于鲁棒控制收缩度量的同伦管方法，实现了对未知非线性系统的约束鲁棒镇定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然双线性Koopman实现比线性形式具有更高的近似精度，但现有鲁棒MPC方法对此建模误差的处理有限。核心难点在于有限维Koopman预测器不保证维持升维状态的流形结构，导致多步预测偏离单步误差证书的有效区域。

Method: 提出将预测的升维状态重新投影回原流形，从而在原状态空间构建误差感知的离散时间控制仿射预测器；随后基于离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量构造同伦管，并设计包含终端成分的管状鲁棒MPC问题。

Result: 证明了真实闭环轨迹对原始非线性约束的鲁棒满足性、递归可行性，以及收敛到目标状态的邻域。数值实验验证了鲁棒镇定效果，并表明所提方法在性能上优于现有基于Koopman的鲁棒MPC方法。

Conclusion: 本文为基于双线性Koopman实现的未知非线性系统鲁棒控制提供了一套完整的理论保证和实用算法框架，有效解决了状态流形偏离问题，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) using Koopman operator theory is a promising approach for constrained control of unknown nonlinear systems. While linear Koopman realizations are commonly used due to their simplicity, bilinear Koopman realizations can provide significantly higher approximation accuracy for nonlinear control systems. However, robust MPC (RMPC) formulations that account for modeling errors in bilinear Koopman realizations remain limited. This paper proposes a RMPC framework for unknown nonlinear systems with general nonlinear constraints based on data-driven bilinear Koopman realizations. A central difficulty is that finite-dimensional Koopman predictors need not preserve the manifold of valid lifted states, so multi-step prediction in lifted coordinates may leave the region where one-step error certificates apply. We address this issue by reprojecting each predicted lifted state back onto the manifold, thereby obtaining an error-aware discrete-time control-affine predictor in the original state space without impractical assumptions. For this predictor, we develop a discrete-time robust control contraction metric based homothetic tube construction, and then formulate a tube-based RMPC problem with terminal ingredients. Under the proposed formulation, we prove robust satisfaction of the original nonlinear constraints by the true closed-loop trajectory, recursive feasibility, and convergence to a neighborhood of the target state. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust stabilization of nonlinear systems and the advantages of the proposed method over existing Koopman-based RMPC approaches in terms of performance.

</details>


### [188] [Face Structure in Partial Facial Reduction of Semidefinite Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25689)
*Hao Hu,Mingming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于可处理内逼近锥（非负对角、对角占优、缩放对角占优）的部分面收缩方法，证明了终端面的结构特征（变量固定为0/1或强制相等），并揭示了该方法的局限性（可达最大步数、无法检测稠密仿射等式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半定松弛常违反Slater条件，全脸收缩虽能恢复严格可行性但代价高昂。本文旨在利用可处理的内逼近锥实现低成本的部分面收缩，并刻画其能恢复的结构与局限性。

Method: 利用非负对角、DD、SDD锥作为PSD锥的可处理内逼近，对半定松弛实施部分面收缩；通过分析终端面的基矩阵结构（{0,1}满列秩、列支撑不交）得到约简形式；构造反例揭示方法局限。

Result: SDD-部分面收缩的终端面中，变量要么固定为0/1，要么被分组强制相等；存在奇异性为2的族，DD/SDD-部分面收缩需最大步数；因子宽度限制导致无法检测稠密有效仿射等式。

Conclusion: 部分面收缩能在低成本下恢复特定结构（0/1固定、分组相等），但在检测密集结构时存在根本性局限，揭示了计算效率与检测能力的权衡。

Abstract: Semidefinite relaxations of binary optimization problems often fail Slater's condition. Full facial reduction restores strict feasibility but may require auxiliary problems as costly as the original relaxation. We analyze partial facial reduction based on tractable inner approximations of the positive semidefinite cone: the nonnegative diagonal, diagonally dominant (DD), and scaled diagonally dominant (SDD) cones.
  For this class of SDP relaxations, we prove that every terminal face identified by SDD-partial FR is described by variables fixed to zero or one and groups of variables forced to be equal. The face admits a full-column-rank \(\{0,1\}\) basis matrix with disjoint column supports, yielding a natural reduced formulation that can preserve sparsity. For polyhedral inner approximations, we characterize the face exposed at each step through the inequalities active on the associated outer relaxation.
  We also establish sharp limitations. We construct a family with singularity degree two for which DD- and SDD-partial FR require the maximum possible number of steps. We further show that factor-width restrictions can prevent detection of dense valid affine equalities. These results characterize both the structure recovered by tractable partial FR and the cost of restricting the exposing-matrix search.

</details>


### [189] [Analysis and Optimal Design of Equilibria in the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25698)
*Alfio Borzì,Gennaro Infante,Giovanni Mascali,Riccardo Scida*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了外电场下平衡粒子分布的最优设计问题，基于Vlasov-Poisson系统的稳态Maxwellian平衡，建立了归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程的适定性理论，并通过Schauder不动点论证和变分法两种途径证明了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性。在此基础上，提出了统一的最优设计框架，涵盖谷势、二次追踪和KL散度三种设计准则，推导了一阶最优性条件，并设计了投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为外电场驱动的粒子平衡分布提供严格的理论基础，并建立统一的最优设计方法论，以解决等离子体或带电粒子系统中平衡密度调控的设计问题。

Method: 通过Schauder不动点论证和基于严格凸自由能泛函的变分法两种互补方法，证明平衡势的存在唯一性；在此基础上将多种设计准则统一纳入优化框架，推导一阶最优性条件，并采用投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程的适定性理论，证明了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性；导出了统一优化框架下的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为等离子体/带电粒子系统中平衡密度的最优设计提供了严格的理论支撑和有效的数值算法，其统一框架可适用于多种设计目标。

Abstract: The optimal design of equilibrium particle distributions generated by external electric fields is investigated in the framework of stationary Maxwellian equilibria of the Vlasov-Poisson system. The resulting normalized Poisson-Boltzmann equation is analyzed, and existence and uniqueness of the self-consistent equilibrium potential are established by two complementary approaches. A Schauder fixed-point argument based on uniform estimates for the normalized nonlinear source and a variational approach based on a strictly convex free-energy functional are developed. Building upon these analytical results, a unified optimal design framework is formulated for equilibrium densities. Valley-potential, quadratic tracking, and Kullback-Leibler design criteria are treated within the same optimization framework. First-order optimality conditions are derived, and a projected nonlinear conjugate-gradient algorithm is proposed for the numerical solution of the resulting optimization problems.

</details>


### [190] [Vertex Loci of Max-Indicator Polytopes and Vertex Retrieval in Ball Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25752)
*Marius Costandin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在球心位于球面且半径相等的条件下，通过最大指示器多面体族和顶点轨迹线，利用线性规划高效检索唯一满足特定条件的顶点的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球交区域$\mathcal{Q}$中，从目标点$C_S$寻找最远点是一个重要问题。已有工作引入最大指示器多面体来刻画该问题的极值点，但尚未提供高效检索这些顶点的算法。本文针对特殊几何设置（球心共球面、半径相等、仅一个顶点满足约束），填补了高效顶点检索的空白。

Method: 首先利用最大指示器多面体的顶点在参数变化时沿通过原点与$\mathcal{Q}$顶点的直线（顶点轨迹线）滑动这一性质。然后对目标点$C_S$沿$1_{n\times1}$方向进行对称扰动，证明两个对称扰动后的多面体的交集严格包含于未扰动的多面体中，且边界公共点仅位于满足$1_{n\times1}^T\cdot x = 0$的顶点与原点的连线上。由此将顶点检索问题转化为线性规划问题。

Result: 在球心共球面、等半径且仅有一个顶点满足$1_{n\times1}\cdot x = 0$的条件下，成功设计出高效算法，能够唯一检索出该顶点。该算法基于线性规划，具有计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入顶点轨迹线和对称扰动技术，将特定几何配置下的最远点顶点检索问题简化为线性规划，为最大指示器多面体的实际应用提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The so called "max-indicator polytopes" were introduced in \cite{mainB} as an intersection of half-spaces related to the problem of finding the farthest in an intersection of balls $\mathcal{Q}$, from a given target point $C_S$. It was shown that if the target point belongs to the convex hull of the balls centers, then a specific intersection of half-spaces forms a single real variable parameterized polytope family which is used to characterized the extreme points.
  This paper, under the assumptions that the intersecting balls centers lie on a sphere and all have equal radii with only one vertex $x$, of $\mathcal{Q}$, meeting $1_{n \times 1}\cdot x = 0$, we provide an efficient algorithm to retrieve that vertex.
  The main idea behind the result is that the max-indicator polytopes vertices, as the family parameter varies, slide on some lines, that we call vertex locii. These lines pass through the space origin and the vertices of $\mathcal{Q}$. Perturbing the target point $C_S$ up and down along the direction $1_{n\times 1}$ we are able to show that the intersection of two simetrically perturbed max-indicator polytopes is strictly included in the unperturbed polytope, with the only boundary common points being on the line connecting the origin with the vertex of $\mathcal{Q}$ meeting $1_{n\times 1}^T\cdot x = 0$. As such, under the said assumptions, the only such vertex can be retrieved using linear programming.

</details>


### [191] [Variance-Reduced Conditional Gradient Methods under Markovian Sampling for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25785)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-ALFCG算法，结合动量条件梯度法与耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛估计，解决基于马尔可夫链轨迹的随机复合非凸优化问题，给出了期望样本复杂度的理论保证，并包含数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轨迹方差缩减理论仅覆盖光滑无约束目标，缺乏对带投影约束的复合非凸优化问题的研究，且实际应用中梯度样本常来自固定遍历马尔可夫链的单一轨迹（如在线学习、强化学习场景）。

Method: 提出MC-ALFCG算法：(1) 使用动量条件梯度法（Frank-Wolfe变体）处理投影-free复合约束；(2) 采用耦合截断多层蒙特卡洛（Coupled Capped Multilevel Monte Carlo）估计，最深嵌套平均使用同一轨迹连续状态；(3) 每步进行裁剪（Clipping）保证路径性有界，以便自适应分析；(4) 将马尔可夫递归约化为独立采样情形，通过引入混合时间参数Λ=O(τ_mix log T)。

Result: 正中心噪声下期望样本复杂度为~O(τ_mix²G_σε⁻³ + τ_mix^{5/2}G_σ²ε⁻³ + τ_mix⁵ε⁻²)；无噪声特例达到~O(ε⁻²)（与混合时间无关常数）；混合时间不可知变体达到~O(τ_mix⁶ε⁻³ + τ_mix³ε⁻²)。所有保证在固定转移核下以期望形式成立。

Conclusion: 首次将单轨迹方差缩减理论推广到复合非凸优化与投影-free设置，MC-ALFCG在马尔可夫噪声下实现了具有显式混合时间依赖的最优或接近最优样本复杂度，且数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We study stochastic composite nonconvex optimization over a compact convex set when gradient samples arrive along a single trajectory of a fixed ergodic Markov chain. Existing single-trajectory variance-reduction theory covers smooth unconstrained objectives; we address the projection-free composite setting using the generalized Frank-Wolfe gap. We propose MC-ALFCG, which combines a momentum conditional-gradient method with coupled capped multilevel Monte Carlo estimation and per-iteration clipping. The deepest nested average uses consecutive states from the same trajectory, yielding conditional bias $O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$ uniformly over the starting state, while coupling controls the gradient-difference second moment through the iterate displacement. Clipping enforces the pathwise bounds needed by the adaptive analysis. We reduce the Markovian recursion to its independent-sampling counterpart under $σ^2\mapsto 2ΛG_σ^2$ and $L^2\mapsto 2ΛL^2$, where $Λ=O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$. For positive centered noise, the tuned method achieves expected sample complexity $\widetilde{O}((τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_σ+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_σ^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$. The exactly noiseless specialization achieves $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ with mixing-time-free constants, while a mixing-time-oblivious variant achieves $\widetilde{O}(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$. All guarantees are in expectation under a fixed transition kernel. Controlled numerical studies examine dependence sensitivity, a nonconvex composite instance, and clipping behavior.

</details>


### [192] [On the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections: perturbations resilience and dynamic string-averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25797)
*Daniel Reem,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Bauschke和Tung（2026）将Meshulam（1996）关于松弛投影无穷乘积有界性结果从有限维推广到无限维Hilbert空间的基础上，进一步推广到扰动情形、混合闭半空间与闭超平面情形，以及动态加权和动态字符串的松弛投影迭代过程，并给出了反例说明推广到任意闭凸集的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bauschke和Tung已将Meshulam关于有限族闭仿射子空间上松弛投影无穷乘积有界性的结果从有限维推广到无限维Hilbert空间，本文旨在从多个方向进一步推广该结果，并探讨其局限性。

Method: 通过引入扰动（证明扰动鲁棒性）、混合闭半空间与闭超平面、以及使用动态加权和动态字符串（动态长度）的松弛投影迭代过程来推广Bauschke-Tung结果；同时构建大族反例展示推广到任意闭凸集的局限性，并建立关于非扩张映射无穷乘积的一致有界性与一致无界性的一般性定理。

Result: 成功在多个方向上推广了Bauschke-Tung结果，包括扰动情形、混合闭半空间与闭超平面情形、动态加权与动态字符串迭代情形；同时发现对于任意闭凸集，若控制序列不是循环甚至不是几乎循环的，则存在反例使得有界性不成立。

Conclusion: Bauschke-Tung结果可进一步推广到扰动、混合几何对象及动态迭代过程，但无法任意推广到一般闭凸集，存在本质局限性。文中还建立了关于非扩张映射无穷乘积的一致有界性/无界性的一般定理，具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Very recently (2026), Bauschke and Tung extended from finite- to infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces a result published by Meshulam in 1996 (following an earlier result of Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb from 1984) regarding the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections onto a finite family of closed affine subspaces. In the present note we extend in various ways the result of Bauschke and Tung by allowing certain perturbations and proving perturbation resilience, by considering a mixture of closed half-spaces and closed hyperplanes, and by using dynamic weighted sums of dynamic strings (of dynamic lengths) of relaxed projections in the iterative process. We also discuss the limitation to generalize the Bauschke-Tung result to arbitrary closed and convex sets by presenting a large family of counterexamples in which the associated control is not cyclic and not even almost cyclic. Along the way we establish a general theorem of independent interest regarding the uniform boundedness and uniform unboundedness of infinite products of nonexpansive mappings in a normed space setting.

</details>


### [193] [Optimization under Persistent State-Dependent Bias: Gradient-based Method and Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26032)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Quan Xiao,Tayfun Gokmen,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在更新过程受到持久且状态依赖的偏差影响下，随机梯度下降（SGD）的收敛问题，提出了一种名为Residual Learning的梯度算法，通过将原始问题重构为等价的双层优化问题来消除硬件偏差导致的收敛失败，理论分析证明了算法能找到原始无偏优化问题的解，并量化了响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际硬件实现中，SGD的更新会受到持久且状态依赖的偏差（如响应函数对梯度进行逐分量缩放），导致算法收敛到错误的最优点。现有研究未充分解决这种偏差带来的收敛失败问题，因此需要提出新方法纠正该偏差。

Method: 首先证明带有状态依赖偏差的SGD实际上是在优化一个惩罚问题，其极小点与真实极小点不一致。然后将原始问题转化为等价的**双层优化问题**，提出**Residual Learning**梯度算法，通过双层结构来消除硬件响应函数带来的偏差影响。

Result: 理论分析表明Residual Learning能够在硬件存在缺陷的情况下找到原始无偏优化问题的解。此外，通过定义**硬件条件数**量化了响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响，并证明一般情况下收敛复杂度与硬件条件数呈多项式依赖关系，且这种依赖是不可避免的。

Conclusion: Residual Learning算法有效解决了SGD在硬件偏差下的收敛失败问题，数值实验验证了算法的有效性。硬件条件数是影响收敛复杂度的关键指标，这种多项式依赖在一般情况下是最优的。

Abstract: This paper studies the convergence of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when the implemented updates are subject to a persistent and state-dependent bias, in which the desired update is scaled by response functions component-wise. Our first contribution is to demonstrate that SGD in this setting implicitly optimizes a penalized problem whose minimizer does not coincide with the true minimizer. To mitigate this convergence failure, we reformulate the original task as an equivalent bilevel optimization problem and propose a gradient-based algorithm, termed Residual Learning. Theoretical analysis shows that Residual Learning finds a solution to the original, unbiased optimization problem despite the hardware imperfections. Beyond exact convergence, we quantify how the response functions affect convergence complexity via the hardware condition number and show that a polynomial dependence on it is unavoidable in general, via a construction of a hard instance. The theoretical results are supported by numerical simulations that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [From dense grids to valid inference: Accounting for regularization bias in nonparametric random coefficient models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25416)
*Lingwei Kong,Maximilian Osterhaus,Michael Pen*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文为非参数估计的随机系数分布的平均泛函（如支付意愿均值、平均弹性等）提供了推断方法，建立了惩罚插件估计量的渐近正态性，并提出了考虑正则化偏差的置信区间，通过蒙特卡洛模拟和实证应用验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机系数分布的惩罚固定网格估计（Heiss et al., 2022）虽能非参数估计分布，但缺乏对其平均泛函（如支付意愿、弹性等）的统计推断方法，尤其是如何在高维密集网格下控制正则化偏差并实现有效推断。

Method: 基于惩罚固定网格估计器，构建惩罚插件估计量用于平均泛函估计；推导出该估计量在惩罚伪真值处的渐近正态性；提出考虑正则化偏差的置信区间构造方法；适用于线性和非线性泛函，支持密集网格以减少近似偏差。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示所提置信区间在有限样本中覆盖率接近名义水平且保持信息量；实证应用（出行方式需求）表明灵活的非参数规范相比标准参数模型可产生经济上有意义的差异。

Conclusion: 该方法为随机系数分布的非参数估计提供了有效的统计推断工具，填补了高维网格下正则化偏差处理的理论空白，实证上展示了非参数方法的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper develops an inference procedure for average functionals of random-coefficient distributions, such as mean willingness-to-pay and average elasticities, when the distribution is estimated nonparametrically using the penalized fixed-grid estimator of Heiss, Hetzenecker, and Osterhaus (2022). We establish asymptotic normality of the corresponding penalized plug-in estimator centered at the functional evaluated at the penalized pseudo-true value and propose a confidence interval that accounts for the regularization bias. Our method applies to a broad class of linear and nonlinear functionals and allows researchers to use dense grids to reduce approximation bias while maintaining valid inference. Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed intervals achieve coverage close to the nominal level while remaining informative in finite samples. An empirical application to travel mode demand illustrates that flexible nonparametric specifications can yield economically meaningful differences relative to standard parametric models.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Lloyd's $K$-Means Clustering Algorithm Is Frank-Wolfe in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25190)
*Michael Pokojovy,J. Marcus Jobe,Simon Lacoste-Julien*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了Lloyd K-means算法与Frank-Wolfe算法之间的联系，证明Lloyd算法是FW的特例，并利用FW在凹目标上的最新进展，推导出SSE目标函数达到局部最小值的非渐近O(1/t)收敛率。针对空簇问题，还开发了适用于半光滑目标的FW变体，保持相同收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lloyd's K-means算法虽然被广泛使用，但其理论收敛性质（尤其是非渐近收敛率）尚不明确。作者希望从优化理论角度揭示Lloyd算法的本质，建立其与经典一阶优化方法（Frank-Wolfe）的联系，从而为K-means算法提供更严格的理论保证。

Method: 作者证明Lloyd's K-means是Frank-Wolfe算法的一个特例；利用FW方法在凹目标函数上的最新理论成果，推导出O(1/t)的非渐近收敛率；针对空簇问题（Lloyd贪婪分配可能产生的现象），开发了适用于半光滑目标的FW变体。

Result: 建立了Lloyd算法与FW算法的理论联系；证明了Lloyd算法达到SSE局部最小值的非渐近O(1/t)收敛率；开发的FW变体在处理空簇时仍保持相同的收敛率，且收敛率仅由初始SSE值控制。

Conclusion: 通过将Lloyd算法纳入Frank-Wolfe框架，为K-means聚类提供了更坚实的优化理论基础，并展示了该方法在球面高斯混合模拟和真实图像分割数据集上的有效性。

Abstract: Lloyd's $K$-means algorithm, also known as naïve $K$-means, is a widely used ad hoc optimization heuristic, designed to minimize the sum of squared errors (SSE) across all $K$-partitions of a dataset via iterative cluster refinement. In this work, we establish a novel connection between Lloyd's algorithm and the Frank-Wolfe (FW) algorithm, a prominent first-order method for projection-free optimization. We demonstrate that Lloyd's algorithm is a special case of FW. Leveraging recent advances in FW methods for concave objectives, we derive a non-asymptotic $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ convergence rate to a local minimum of the SSE objective. To account for empty clusters, an outcome possible under Lloyd's greedy assignment, we develop an FW variant for semismooth objectives while retaining the same convergence rate that is solely controlled by the initial SSE value. We illustrate our findings with a simulation study for spherical Gaussian mixtures and a real-world image segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [196] [Learning from the Unseen: Offline Reinforcement Learning with Hidden Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25241)
*Zeyu Bian,Ying Zhou,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线强化学习中动作值被噪声污染的问题，提出了首个针对隐藏动作的离线策略评估方法LURE，通过利用下一状态变量作为代理实现多重鲁棒估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中真实动作常不可观测，仅有带噪声的代理，导致现有离线RL方法产生有偏结论。作者希望解决这一被忽视的问题。

Method: 利用下一状态变量作为未观测动作的自然代理，建立策略值的可识别性；提出基于影响函数的估计器LURE，实现多重鲁棒性和渐近正态性。

Result: LURE在多种正确设定的 nuisance 组件组合下保持一致估计，在仿真和MIMIC-III脓毒症管理应用中表现有效。

Conclusion: 这是首个处理隐藏动作离线RL的工作，为带噪声动作数据的策略评估提供了可行的统计推断工具。

Abstract: Standard offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms typically assume that the actions in the dataset are observed without error. However, in many real-world applications, the true actions are unobserved and only noisy proxies are available, causing existing RL methods to yield biased and potentially misleading conclusions. We study off-policy evaluation in infinite-horizon discounted Markov decision processes with hidden actions. By leveraging the next-state variable as a natural proxy for the unobserved action, we establish identification of the policy value and propose an influence-function-based estimator called LURE (Learning from the Unseen: Robust Estimator). LURE is multiply robust, remaining consistent under several combinations of correctly specified nuisance components, and is asymptotically normal, enabling valid statistical inference. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address offline RL with hidden actions. We demonstrate LURE's effectiveness through simulations and a sepsis management application using the MIMIC-III database.

</details>


### [197] [Can Deep Generative Models Reproduce Non-Stationary Gaussian Random Fields?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25929)
*Daniel Kua,Yan Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种深度生成模型（流匹配、DDPM、score-SDE、VAE）在已知非平稳高斯随机场上的表现，发现所有模型都能恢复均值表面，但在协方差恢复上表现各异：DDPM和score-SDE表现较好，流匹配存在轻微偏差，VAE则难以恢复协方差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型越来越多用于空间/时空建模，但评估其是否学习到真实数据生成过程十分困难，因为真实分布未知且评估常仅依赖观测数据。本文旨在建立一套系统的评估框架和指标，以量化DGM对真实统计结构的恢复能力。

Method: 在已知理论统计量（均值、协方差）的非平稳高斯随机场上，分别训练流匹配、DDPM、score-SDE和VAE四种模型，使用全面指标（包括对比真实样本和理论值）评估均值面和协方差结构的恢复效果，并以ERA5温度异常数据作为实际应用验证。

Result: 四种模型均能有效恢复均值面。协方差恢复方面，DDPM和score-SDE表现较好；流匹配出现轻微的非平稳性衰减和方差欠分散；VAE则难以恢复协方差结构。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个可验证的评估框架，能有效区分不同DGM在统计结构恢复上的能力差异，为实际复杂时空数据的模型选择和开发提供指导。

Abstract: Deep generative models (DGMs) are widely used for complex high-dimensional data and increasingly applied to spatial and spatio-temporal modeling. Their generated samples implicitly represent the learned data distribution and associated uncertainty. However, for real-world data, assessing whether DGMs have learned the underlying process is difficult because the ground truth is unknown and evaluation often relies on observations alone. We evaluate representative DGMs, flow matching (FM), DDPM, score-SDE, and VAE, on a known non-stationary Gaussian random field. This paper provides comprehensive metrics to assess recovery of the ground-truth mean and covariance structures, with oracle samples and a stationary control as references. All four models recover the mean surface, while their covariance recovery differs across model families: DDPM and score-SDE recover the covariance structure reasonably well, FM exhibits mildly attenuated non-stationarity and slight variance under-dispersion, and VAE has difficulty recovering the covariance structure. An experiment on ERA5 temperature anomalies further demonstrates how the framework can support the validation and development of DGMs for complex real-world spatio-temporal data.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Long-memory GARCH via a two-dimensional Markov chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25189)
*Kyungsub Lee,Kennedy Titus Kayaki*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种GARCH型波动率模型，通过潜幂律核的水平和斜率更新实现二维马尔可夫状态下过去冲击的状态依赖衰减，并证明了正Harris递归和不变分布唯一性。模拟显示对数平方创新中存在显著低频持续性，实证表明该模型仅用二维马尔可夫状态就能捕捉大部分波动率持续性并提供有竞争力的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GARCH模型在刻画波动率的长期记忆性和状态依赖衰减方面存在局限，作者希望设计一种更简洁但能捕捉低频持续性的波动率模型。

Method: 提出一种GARCH型波动率模型，通过潜幂律核的水平和斜率更新生成二维马尔可夫状态，使过去冲击的衰减具有状态依赖性。推导了联合Foster-Lyapunov条件以证明正Harris递归和不变分布唯一性。

Result: 模拟显示对数平方创新在诊断稳定性边界附近表现出显著的低频持续性。实证中，仅用二维马尔可夫状态就捕捉了大部分观察到的波动率持续性，并取得了有竞争力的样本外预测精度。

Conclusion: 该模型在保持低维状态的同时，成功刻画了波动率的长期记忆特性，在理论和实证上均表现良好。

Abstract: This paper proposes a GARCH-type volatility model in which level-and-slope updates of a latent power-law kernel generate state-dependent decay of past shocks within a two-dimensional Markov state. We derive a joint Foster--Lyapunov condition and establish positive Harris recurrence and uniqueness of the invariant distribution. Simulations show substantial low-frequency persistence in log-squared innovations, especially near the diagnostic stability boundary. Empirically, the model captures a substantial portion of observed volatility persistence and delivers competitive out-of-sample forecast accuracy using only a two-dimensional Markov state.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.RM'></div>

# q-fin.RM [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Robust Hedging Valuation Adjustment for Deep Hedging Policies under Market Frictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25258)
*Takayuki Sakuma*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合鲁棒对冲估值调整（HVA）的深度对冲后训练框架，将资金与保证金附加项纳入同一KL不确定性集，实现单一压力倾斜联合计算三项指标，并在不同流动性市场下比较了经典对冲策略与学习型策略的表现，发现没有一种策略在所有市场中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交易成本与市场摩擦条件下，传统深度对冲训练出的神经网络策略无法评估交易台是否承担得起该策略，缺乏对资金与保证金成本的统一考量。

Method: 在深度对冲后引入鲁棒HVA作为估值调整层，与资金和保证金附加项共享同一KL不确定性集，通过单一共同压力倾斜联合计算三项指标，并比较经典对冲策略与学习型策略在三种流动性市场下的表现。

Result: 没有一种策略在所有市场中占优。严格跟踪风险预算下，高/中流动性市场选择伽马宽经典带策略，低流动性市场选择稀疏学习执行策略；宽松验证预算下更宽经典带策略普遍被选中。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够为交易台提供内部一致的对冲储备，避免分别计算三项指标带来的不一致性，且策略选择需根据市场流动性条件灵活调整。

Abstract: Hedging a derivative position under transaction costs and market frictions requires a trading rule that adapts to changing conditions. Deep hedging trains a neural policy for this task but policy training does not determine whether a trading desk can afford to run the policy. We apply robust hedging valuation adjustment (HVA) as a post-training valuation-adjustment layer that evaluates tracking-loss CVaR together with explicit funding and margin add-ons. The funding and margin add-ons share the same KL uncertainty set as HVA. For each policy, a single common-stress tilt computes HVA, funding and margin jointly and a trading desk can get one internally consistent reserve instead of the three separately. We compare classical hedge policies with learned hedge specifications across three market environments with different liquidity. No single specification dominates in every market. Under the strict tracking-risk budget, gamma-wide classical bands are selected in High and Middle Liquidity while sparse learned execution is selected in Low Liquidity. At looser validation budgets wider classical bands are generally selected.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FinAbstain框架，通过校准的不确定性估计实现多模态RAG的选择性预测，在金融预测中通过"弃权"机制平衡覆盖率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在金融预测中容易在证据稀疏、过时或矛盾时表达高置信度，导致错误预测。

Method: 构建时间点检索器+多模态智能体（基本面、新闻、技术、风险、验证），聚合概率评估，使用温度缩放、保序回归、共形预测及混合不确定性评分，控制器在不确定性过高时弃权。

Result: 模拟结果显示校准后的弃权机制能以覆盖率换取更低的错误率和回撤。

Conclusion: 贡献是时间安全架构、复合不确定性公式以及可复现的金融选择性预测评估蓝图。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>


### [201] [Emergent Latent-State Computation under Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25459)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Lulu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列模型在部分可观测、含噪的潜在随机动力学中的内部表征机制，发现两阶段计算模式（隐状态编码→输出映射），并为Transformer在不同波动周期下的隐状态解码提供了可解释的结构证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机械可解释性主要聚焦于语言模型和确定性玩具任务，缺乏对序列模型如何内部表征含噪、部分可观测的潜在随机动力学的研究。作者希望在受控的随机波动率环境下填补这一空白。

Method: 在多元随机波动率 (stochastic volatility) 设定下，模型仅观测收益率，而真实潜在波动率状态已知。通过跨架构、损失函数和输出头分析，探测隐藏表征是否编码了下一时刻的潜在波动率状态，并观察Transformer中可解码性出现的位置与波动周期的关系。

Result: 1. 隐藏表征包含关于下一潜在波动率状态的大量信息；2. 输出头将表征映射到平方收益预测；3. Transformer中隐状态可解码性出现在特定架构阶段，位置取决于波动周期；4. 长周期下，计算简化为显式潜在状态滤波器（学习到的线性投影+ℓ²归一化）；5. 噪声MSE训练下的部分性能下降源于读出对齐问题而非表征失败。

Conclusion: 随机波动率模型为研究含噪潜在动力学和部分可观测性下的机械可解释性提供了一个有用的基准测试框架。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks. Much less is known about how sequence models internally represent latent stochastic dynamics under noisy, partially observed observations. We study this question in a controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting, where models observe only returns while the ground-truth latent volatility state is known to the researcher. This setting provides a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under partial observability: the latent state is hidden from the model but directly available for evaluation. Across architectures, losses, and output heads, we find evidence for a two-stage computation. Hidden representations encode substantial information about the next latent volatility state, and the output head maps this representation to squared return forecasts. Furthermore, in Transformers, latent-state decodability emerges at identifiable architectural stages whose location depends on the volatility period. In long-cycle regimes, this computation simplifies into an explicit latent-state filter consisting of a learned linear projection followed by $\ell^2$ normalization. Output-head replacement further shows that part of the degradation under noisy MSE training arises from readout misalignment rather than representation failure. These results suggest that stochastic volatility models provide a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under noisy latent dynamics and partial observability.

</details>


### [202] [Human Preference aligned Tabular Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24880)
*Frederik Hoppe,Astrid Franz,Marianne Michaelis,Lars Kleinemeier,Udo Göbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有的表格嵌入方法主要针对预测任务优化，而非人类偏好对齐的相似性排序，并提出需要引入人类偏好对齐评估来评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度，通过PLM用例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格嵌入方法在相似性搜索中的评估标准（下游任务指标）不足以衡量其与人类偏好对齐的程度，缺乏对人类偏好对齐的评估体系。

Method: 提出了一套具体的评估流程，通过产品生命周期管理（PLM）用例来展示问题的存在。

Result: 通过PLM用例验证了现有嵌入方法在人类偏好对齐方面存在不足，标准下游指标无法全面评估嵌入的可信度。

Conclusion: 人类偏好对齐评估是评估表格嵌入在相似性搜索中可信度的必要组成部分，现有评估体系需要补充该维度。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [203] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出行为驱动可解释性（BDX）方法，利用行为驱动开发（BDD）的结构化场景描述系统功能，从而生成系统行为的自然语言解释，并通过RISC-V处理器异常案例验证了该方法在系统设计阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着系统复杂性急剧增加，单个人员或团队难以完全理解系统开发全生命周期（如设计、维护等）的各个方面。尤其对于安全关键系统，最终设计的可信度至关重要，因此可解释性成为现代系统的重要需求。

Method: 利用行为驱动开发（BDD）中描述预期系统行为的结构化场景（scenarios），这些场景为每个功能提供一系列动作序列，可直接转化为自然语言解释。作者将此方法称为行为驱动可解释性（BDX），可适用于任何开发阶段或抽象层级。

Result: 通过RISC-V处理器中异常解释的案例研究，展示了BDX在系统设计阶段对可解释性的支持作用。

Conclusion: BDX方法通过BDD场景生成解释，增强了系统设计的可信度，且具有跨开发阶段和抽象层级的通用适用性。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [204] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了无标签分类（CWoLa）从二分类到多分类场景，证明在仅知混合身份而不知类标签和混合比例的情况下，通过训练分类器区分不同未标记混合样本，可以恢复潜在类结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多分类问题中，可靠的实例级标签不可用，但可以构建弱富集的未标记样本（不同筛选、来源、群体或实验条件下的数据集）。现有CWoLa方法仅适用于二分类（K=2），需要将其推广到更通用的多分类场景（K>2）。

Method: 证明多分类混合模型中，贝叶斯最优混合分类器将数据点映射到嵌入在混合后验空间中的(K-1)维单纯形，其K个顶点由潜在类通过未知混合矩阵诱导。提出两种先验无关方法：后验单纯形拟合和瓶颈架构，通过训练标准分类器区分混合身份来提取潜在类结构。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上的实验表明，仅利用混合身份即可恢复潜在类及其在混合中的比例，缩小了弱监督与全监督性能之间的差距。

Conclusion: 提供了一种数学上合理、可扩展的多类别发现工具，适用于标签稀缺领域，弥合了弱监督与全监督学习之间的差距。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [205] [Eliminating Propagation Delay: Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24885)
*Jinpeng Chen,Ziyu Yu,Tao Wang,Jun Ma,Hongbo Gao,Senzhang Wang,Zufeng Zhang,Kaimin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A-STFGCN，通过空间-时间融合块消除传播延迟误差，并利用基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕捉长短期时间特征，在五个真实数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图卷积模型忽略了相邻节点间信息传播延迟差异，且复杂结构导致计算时间增加，无法满足模型时效性需求。

Method: 提出A-STFGCN网络，设计空间-时间融合块去除传播延迟误差，基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕捉长短期时间特征。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，与八种基线方法相比，该方法在整体性能、计算和数据利用效率方面均达到最优。

Conclusion: A-STFGCN有效解决了传播延迟问题并降低了计算复杂度，是一种兼具精度和效率的交通流预测方法。

Abstract: Predicting traffic flow is crucial to optimizing transportation systems and improving urban mobility. Many graph convolution-based models have been proposed to extract spatial-temporal features and predict traffic flow. However, most focus on spatial-temporal and semantic correlation in topological relationships. There are two primary problems to address. Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Secondly, these methods often stack a large number of complex structures, resulting in a substantial increase in computational time during the model training phase, thereby disregarding the model's requirements for timeliness. In this paper, we propose a novel network called the Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN). We design a spatial-temporal fusion block to extract the spatial-temporal feature correlations with propagation delay errors removed and to capture both long-term and short-term temporal characteristics of the data within a multi-head self-attention mechanism based on a mask matrix. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves the best overall performance while having good computation and data utilization efficiency compared with the eight baseline methods.

</details>


### [206] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDRO框架，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对方法，在无需似然访问的条件下实现基于任意条件生成器的分布鲁棒优化，并在库存管理和社交导航任务中显著降低罕见场景下的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式分布鲁棒优化方法在模型兼容性与对抗结构之间存在权衡：接受任意采样器的方法无法限制最坏情况分布到生成器族，而生成器参数化的对抗方法又依赖似然、评分或训练数据等模型特定访问。需要一种既能接受任意可采样条件生成器作为名义模型，又能将最坏情况分布限制在选定条件生成器族内的通用框架。

Method: 提出GDRO框架，核心是采样器-Sinkhorn配对：采样器精确表示条件分布，Sinkhorn散度无需似然访问即可比较分布，且可从样本中估计。该框架在人口问题中允许直接有限样本近似和可微的原始-对偶实现。

Result: 在显式和隐式生成器上，相比名义决策，该方法在库存管理中罕见上下文遗憾降低60%，SocialGAN导航碰撞减少50%。对于Lipschitz损失，人口Sinkhorn半径可约束下游性能退化。

Conclusion: GDRO成功解决了生成式DRO中模型兼容性与对抗结构的权衡问题，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对实现了对任意条件生成器的支持，并有效提升了罕见场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [207] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效对齐维度（effective alignment dimension）来量化残差扩展中训练-测试梯度对齐的信号-噪声几何，推导了有限样本下的错位概率上界，并在多个模型（LLaMA、Pythia、ResNet-20）上验证了该理论对测试损失变化的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络宽度理论主要描述渐近极限，对于从有限训练数据中识别出的扩展方向能否在未见数据上保持有效，缺乏实用指导。

Method: 提出有效对齐维度，推导独立估计的训练梯度与测试梯度内积的精确均值与方差，得到有限样本下的错位概率上界，该上界仅依赖于有效对齐维度和有效样本量，无需协方差谱假设或预设宽度增长率。

Result: 在LLaMA式Transformer、Pythia和ResNet-20上的实验表明，更宽的模型具有更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验错位率。直接残差干预实验证实，对齐统计量能预测留出集损失变化的符号与幅度。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为有限数据下残差扩展的安全性提供了可计算的保障条件，建立了宽度、对齐维度与泛化性能之间的可操作联系。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [208] [Algorithmic Separation between Constant-Depth and Logarithmic-Depth Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25200)
*Yunwei Ren,Zihao Wang,Jason D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了对数深度网络在算法上能够学习常数深度网络无法有效学习的布尔函数类，实现了深度分离的算法性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络相比浅层网络具有经验优势，但理论上的深度分离主要局限于逼近能力，而算法层面的结果大多仅限于两层与三层网络的比较，缺乏对更深层网络算法优势的理解。

Method: 识别了一类具有层次化傅里叶谱结构的布尔函数，对数深度网络可通过逐层坐标下降法分层自适应重建谱来高效学习；同时构造了子类，证明常数深度多项式宽度网络在均匀分布下会 incur 常数L2逼近误差。

Result: 证明了对数深度网络与常数深度网络之间存在算法上的分离：前者可高效学习特定布尔函数类，后者即使有足够正则的激活函数和控制谱范数也无法实现低误差逼近。

Conclusion: 首次在算法层面证明了对数深度网络优于常数深度网络，深化了对深度网络层次结构优势的理论理解。

Abstract: Despite the empirical advantages of deep networks over shallow ones, theoretical depth separations largely concern approximation power, while algorithmic results are mostly limited to comparisons between two- and three-layer networks. In this work, we prove the first algorithmic separation between constant-depth and logarithmic-depth networks.
  Specifically, we identify a class of Boolean functions with hierarchically structured Fourier spectra that logarithmic-depth networks can learn efficiently using layerwise coordinate descent by reconstructing the spectra hierarchically and adaptively. We also exhibit a subclass for which every constant-depth, polynomial-width network with sufficiently regular activations and controlled spectral norms must incur constant $L^2$ approximation error under the uniform distribution over the hypercube.

</details>


### [209] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GAUGE基准，用于评估AI构建的估值模型。与传统单点评分不同，GAUGE基于1,001份分析师工作簿建立三层实践包络，发现分析师间认同度低（中位数0.33），而最佳AI代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有高级分析师，模型构建能力强但判断力弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常以单一专家答案作为标准评分，但财务模型中的预测、折现率、目标价等本就有多种合理答案，分析师之间也存在大量分歧。因此需要一种能反映实践中合理分歧范围的评估方法。

Method: 基于1,001份分析师工作簿构建三层观测实践包络（observed-practice envelope），包含196个评估任务、56个可审计维度、8个有效性门控和确定性结构检查。采用55人已知群体研究、公司分组交叉验证和判断者稳定性审计来验证基准。

Result: 在失败感知分数φ₀上，高级分析师平均88.3，初级66.0，金融学生43.2。最佳AI代理得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有高级分析师和大多数初级分析师。代理通过93%的机械维度和78%的判断维度，但集群中间差距达26分。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在模型构建方面远强于估值判断。论文公开了方法论、去标识数据、训练集、48任务评估核心及刷新池，为估值模型评估提供了更符合实践的标准。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [210] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contextual Deconvolution（CD）方法，通过凸分解将促销冲击与平滑基线分离，在M5和Favorita数据集上降低安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，但仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时才能降低总成本。CD的主要贡献在于预测可靠性而非中心趋势，在所有基线中实现了最低的跨截面误差离散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习需求预测虽优化了统计精度，但留下了过多的操作波动性，导致安全库存膨胀并加剧牛鞭效应。作者希望设计一种既能保持预测精度、又能减少操作波动性的方法。

Method: 提出Contextual Deconvolution两阶段估计器：1) 通过核调制带状算子将促销驱动的瞬时冲击从平滑结构基线中分离出来；2) 采用层次化部分池化实现目录级部署，无需对每个SKU单独训练。

Result: 在30,490个M5 SKU和2,845个Favorita商品上评估：CD降低了安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，但在促销事件期间供应不足。仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%（95% CI [17%,25%]）时才能降低总成本。CD在所有基线中取得了最低的跨截面误差离散度，在0.8%的SKU上出现严重误预测，而基线为9.9-20.6%。

Conclusion: CD的主要贡献是提高预测可靠性而非中心趋势；当持有成本较小时，它主要作为操作稳定性和库存资本层发挥作用，而非预期成本最小化器。学习到的需求算子是非正态的，但CD的紧凑参数化核能以可解释的方式匹配其操作性能。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [211] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFP框架，将文本条件时间序列预测建模为规划问题，利用冻结的TSFM作为模拟器、LLM作为策略和价值函数，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索实现无训练跨模态预测，实验证明该方法在不同模型组合上均有一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法让LLM直接生成或修正预测值会破坏TSFM捕获的时间结构，需要一种能结合两者优势且不破坏时间序列特征的训练-free方法。

Method: 提出LAFP框架：(1) 冻结的TSFM作为模拟器生成数值轨迹；(2) LLM分为Ranker（作为策略函数，引导候选选择）和Judge（作为价值函数，评估完整轨迹是否符合文本上下文）；(3) 使用蒙特卡洛树搜索在预测时域上搜索最优轨迹。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD数据集上，使用两种TSFM（Chronos、TimesFM）和四种LLM进行实验，LAFP在所有模型组合下均获得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 序列搜索是一种有效的无训练文本条件预测方法，成功桥接了TSFM和LLM的模态鸿沟，无需对任一模型进行重新训练。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [212] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将矩阵感知几何引入SAM优化器，对矩阵值隐藏层参数使用谱内扰动，并结合Muon外更新，在ImageNet-1K上ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50上取得最优验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM变体虽探索了多种几何选择，但哪种最有效仍不明确。受Muon优化器启发，研究矩阵感知几何在SAM两阶段中的作用。

Method: 在SAM的内阶段引入层状谱内扰动用于矩阵值隐藏层参数，外阶段结合AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器进行更新。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，谱内扰动+Muon外更新的组合在ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50上均取得最佳验证准确率。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知几何（谱内扰动+Muon外更新）是SAM优化中更有效的方案。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [213] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PARED方法，从演示数据中恢复隐式奖励函数，无需偏好标注即可用于语言模型对齐，通过轻量级判别器在特征空间区分演示与策略样本，实验证明其可用于推理时重排序和在线强化学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型对齐方法主要依赖监督微调或强化学习（需奖励模型/人类反馈），但未充分探索一个问题：仅凭演示数据能否提取出可检查、可重用、可在线优化的隐式奖励，从而实现无需偏好标注的AI对齐。

Method: 受逆向强化学习启发，PARED通过一个轻量级判别器，在少量响应级特征空间中区分专家演示与策略自身采样，从而将演示背后的隐式奖励恢复为显式函数。无需任务特定的偏好标注，演示提供任务监督，可结合AI反馈作为额外监督维度。

Result: 实验表明：1) 恢复的奖励可用于推理时重排序和对抗性在线强化学习；2) 能在无监督损失下改进基础策略；3) 在标准SFT后进一步优化可带来额外收益；4) 可应用于上下文对齐，即单一策略可根据不同受众偏好进行定制。

Conclusion: PARED证明了从演示中恢复隐式奖励的有效性，为语言模型对齐提供了一种无需偏好标注的新范式，并展示了奖励在重排序、在线优化和上下文对齐等场景中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [214] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的非对称表示学习目标（Reinformed Dreamer），通过在潜在空间中使用引导信号改进了基于模型的强化学习算法，相比之前的非对称方法（如Informed Dreamer），在多个基准测试中表现出更一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对称强化学习方法（如Informed Dreamer）在学习特权信息表示时存在局限性，无法充分利用额外监督信息来改善表示质量和行为学习。

Method: 提出Reinformed Dreamer，引入基于潜在引导（latent guidance）的新型非对称表示学习目标，在基于模型的强化学习框架中改进观测表示和特权信息表示的学习。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，Reinformed Dreamer相比Dreamer和之前的非对称方法表现出更一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过潜在引导的非对称表示学习可以有效克服Informed Dreamer的表示学习局限，为基于模型的强化学习提供更稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning.
